**Two-bound constraints (registered as formulas, not constants):** `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` in `[ceil_div(TICK_RATE, FLASH_MAX_PER_S), 40]`; `HONEST_TICK_MS` pinned at 500 (30 ticks; the validator cross-checks it against D.2's `honest_tick_floor_ms` and the window test at the live `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` with `u` = 1, and fails the data file if the pinned grid is infeasible there); `FLASH_AREA_EXEMPT_PM` in `[50, 100]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; `BAR_LEN_*_PX` ordered S < M < L. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Not a knob:** the strip cap (12 % x 5 %), the safe margin (5 %), the palette, the glyph size (16 x 16) and the numeral floor (7 art px) are the art bible's and are never tuned here.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

The art bible section 7 (7.0 "the HUD is a suit, the ship is an instrument", 7.1 to 7.3, and the hard requirements HR-1 to HR-15 of 7.7) is the spec of record; this section turns it into per-event visual rows, a palette rule set, a geometry table, a lie-slot specification and an asset inventory. The art-director was consulted (proposal reconciled below; where a figure in that proposal had been overtaken by a ruling, the ruling's figure is used and the change is noted in line). Row ids are `HSV-n`. "HUD px" is one pixel of the HUD canvas (`H` screen px, Pipeline D.4); at `H_default` on a 1080p screen it equals one base art px, so the art bible's "art px" and the HUD px coincide at the default and nothing re-authors at higher `H`. Every size below is in HUD px. The HUD plays no sound of its own; audio appears here only as the coupling the visual must keep (names only; Adaptive Audio & Tells owns every sound).

### Art-bible principles that bind this system

| Art-bible reference | Principle | How it lands on the HUD | Test that proves it |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Corollary | When the game lies, the lie leaks; the frame is an anchor | The frame is fed nothing by the Filter; only the five readings and the scanner slot (and the one-bit `suit.alarm` key in its mark slot) are fed `perceived(...)` per render frame | Frame register bit-identical with the Filter on or off (AC-HS-88) |
| 1 Principle 1 | Darkness is honest; light is a decision | No HUD element reveals what the beam has not lit; a prompt exists only where Interaction says the subject is lit | No prompt over an unlit subject (AC-HS-64) |
| 1 Principle 2 | State lives in the sprite, not the icon | No ship-state icon; the "COOLANT LOW" banner is rejected by the bible itself (ab:742) | Forbidden-surface lint (AC-HS-7) |
| 1 Principle 3 | Emission is exposure | The HUD shows the lamp **switch** (the wearer's own equipment) and never illumination or exposure (HS-19) | Lint for `exposed`, `exposure`, `lit_state` (AC-HS-6) |
| 3.4 | The HUD is a human instrument: chamfers, bezels, rivet lines; the only curves are dial arcs and needles; frames solid, heavy, static; readings thin, light, moving | Every corner a chamfer; the 3.4 split is the luminance rule in "Palette and role rules" below; "heavy" for a frame is carried by mass (chamfer, rivets, plate area), not by stroke width (the bible's wording note is in the amendments table) | Art review of the plate set |
| 3.5 | The beam is the brightest hero | The HUD is exempt by layer (HR-1); the non-Ground area cap keeps the exemption honest (HS-56) | `nonground_count` (AC-HS-81) |
| 4.1, 4.2 | Palette; Alarm Red only for alarm; a loop hue only while its loop runs | The seven-colour rule below | Pixel-colour scan (AC-HS-30, AC-HS-101) |
| 4.4 | Violet and Flesh are reserved for sanity leaks; the gleam is the only violet in screen space | The gleam in the exact fringe `#B07BE6`, dormant at MVP; zero violet pixels on the HUD at MVP | Pixel-band audit on retained screenshots (AC-HS-42) |
| 4.5 | Luminance ladder | Rules L-1 to L-5 below | Greyscale review of the retained screenshots |
| 4.6, 4.7 | UI palette; colour-blind table | Every colour has a shape backup | CVD and achromatopsia captures in the evidence folder |
| 5.1 | The tool layer is an anchor drawn once | Rail glyphs are derived from the tool sprite profiles at 14 x 14 live inside a 16 x 16 cell; no second tool vocabulary | Profile-derivation review |
| 7.0 to 7.3 | The spec of record | Throughout | HR map below |
| 8.5 | Naming | Asset stems follow `ui_<subject>_<variant>_<size>` as the Suit GDD already used | Asset lint |
| 2.9 (cited) | Sanity must not be readable off screen energy | No HUD element gains energy, speed or contrast as sanity falls | Sanity's retained-screenshot criterion (AC-SN-83) |
| 2.10 (cited) | Loss: no death screen | HSV-18 | AC-HS-86 |

### Truth-channel classification

Every visual the HUD emits is outside the Graph's closed **consequence channel** (the HUD writes nothing to the simulation and neither adds nor removes a consequence cue). Within the HUD there are **four classes** (A, M, T, R; the earlier text said three while listing four) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*:

- **ANCHOR (A):** never shifts under hallucination; fed nothing by the Filter; the HR-6 list; bit-identical with the Filter on or off.
- **META-TRUTH (M):** honest by definition, in the meta register; never lie-capable (no gleam, glide, tick or stale). Prompts, the channel bar (the actor's own elapsed time) and the two notices.
- **TRUTH READING (T):** a Graph-driven one-bit reading drawn in a mark slot (the ship-alarm mark `suit.alarm`, accepted at MVP by user ruling D89, HS-25, HS-30) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. It is fed through the Filter like a reading but its lie stage is pinned `never` permanently (the same mechanism as `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE` at MVP, here without a stage at which it turns on), so it has no gleam, motion or sound-request slot and is checked by the Filter-immunity lint as a pinned-never key.
- **READING (R):** lie-capable instrument pixels, fed by `perceived(SUIT_KEY(k), PlayerId)` or `perceived(SCANNER, PlayerId)` every render frame and never cached. Honest at MVP (`READING_LIE_STAGE` is "never" for every key, `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE` is never), but built with dormant gleam, motion and sound-request slots (HS-26).

| Visual | Class | Note |
|---|---|---|
| Strip, panel and rail plates, bezels, chamfers, corner rivets, dial faces, zone-band patterns, edge ticks, pivot caps | A | The dial face is fixed art; only the needle position is the reading |
| Breath-dial needle and numeral (strip): the ambient O2 in ROOM, GASP and NONE, the reserve in RESERVE (HS-12) | R | The strip arc glass is the gleam host. Which family it shows is chosen by the published `breath_src` (class A); the pixels are the shown family's `Perceived` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| Strip engraved layers (dkPa zone notches; tank tick and warn notch) and their lit-state | A | Fixed engraving; the lit-state follows `breath_src` only (HSV-4, HSV-42), never a reading *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| Reserve numeral | R | The reserve glyph's silhouette is A; its hue state is a function of the published `breath_src` (class A, HS-20, HSV-4), not of the reading *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| Integrity needle, warmth needle, charge fill level | R | The charge fill has no needle (CF-HS-14) |
| Zone-band edge ticks | A | The dial's engraving; a lying needle crossing it is the point |
| Warning mark (suit-rung slots) | R by inheritance | The mark's sprite and clock are A; whether it shows is the reading's class (HS-30). At MVP the Suit's rungs drive it. Wording change routed to Sanity SN-19 (CF-HS-19, OQ-HS-61); nothing asserts the Vertical Slice A behaviour |
| Warning mark (ship alarm, `suit.alarm`, accepted at MVP) | T (pinned-never Filter key, honest by source) | Graph `alarm_eligible` only; never shown, suppressed or tinted by a lie (HS-30); never names a loop or a place |
| Lamp-switch outline on the charge cell | A | The wearer's own equipment, not a reading of the world |
| Tool glyphs, equipped bracket, keycaps, glowstick count, carried-module glyph (the suppressant's pips return with the tool, X6) | A | Rail counts are anchors (HS-34) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| Scanner reading slot | R | The plate and the scanner glyph around it are A |
| Prompt plate, keycap, verb text, anchor tab, NEEDS_TOOL notch | M | Interaction's contract |
| Channel bar, needle-mark, break-notch, drain, plate dim | M | |
| Failed-write notice, first-frame line | M | Meta-honest |
| Reserved crew-strip, chat, caption and own-identity anchors | A (empty at MVP) | Draw nothing |

### Palette and role rules

**The MVP HUD uses seven colours:** Ground `#1C2128`, Graphite lit `#5C6670`, Hull Grey `#8C949C`, Paper White `#DDE4EA`, Tungsten fixture `#FFCC66`, O2 Cyan `#3FD6E8`, Alarm Red `#E0102A`; plus the dormant gleam fringe `#B07BE6`. Graphite mid (art bible 4.1) is used for exactly one thing, the one-step plate dim of the OUTCOME frame (HSV-29). No other loop hue appears on the MVP HUD: Power Amber, Coolant Ice and Fuel Ember have no suit reading because the HUD shows no ship state, so the Flesh-band risk of Fuel Ember and the Amber/Tungsten collision (ab 4.2) cannot occur on the HUD. Any HUD colour outside the seven plus the fringe is a defect (AC-HS-30).

| Element | Colour | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| Plate interior (every reading, glyph, label, prompt) | Ground | Opaque (HR-2) |
| Plate border (1 HUD px) | Graphite lit | Static; never a fill behind type |
| Corner rivet (2 x 2, one per bezel corner) | Hull Grey | "Fastener rhythm", never a texture; Hull Grey sits only on Ground or the border row, never on a Graphite-lit fill (HR-2) |
| Frame labels (gauge names) | Hull Grey, 5-cap | Engraved by colour, not weight (7.1) |
| Needles, numerals, keycap letters, verb text | Paper White | The brightest ink on the HUD (L* about 90) |
| O2 ring-clamp glyph (reserve row), strip arc band and pivot cap | O2 Cyan while a breathable source or reserve exists, else Graphite lit | The glyph never swaps shape for state (HS-20) |
| Integrity glyph (rivet line) | Hull Grey | The suit is alive whenever the HUD draws |
| Warmth glyph | Paper White | "A suit reading without a loop": no loop hue (HS-21) |
| Charge cell | Outline Paper White, fill Tungsten fixture; lamp off: outline Graphite lit, fill held | The lamp switch is outline luminance (L* 90 against 43); the `toggle_beam` keycap sits beside it |
| Tool glyphs | Paper White; equipped = Tungsten fixture plus the HR-11 bracket | Focus is a shape |
| Warning mark | Alarm Red fill, Paper White exclamation stroke | The only Alarm Red on the HUD; never text |
| Bar fill | Tungsten fixture, stepped rectangles | Every verb; never Alarm Red, Violet, Flesh, a loop hue or Chemlight |
| Bar needle-mark | Paper White 2 x 8 | Overhangs the 6-px track by 1 px above and below, so shape (not colour) separates it from the Tungsten fill (Paper White against Tungsten is about 1.2:1) |
| Focus bracket | Tungsten plus one pixel of bezel thickening | HR-11 |
| Notice glyph and text | Paper White on Ground | No Alarm Red, Flesh or Violet |
| Glowstick glyph | Paper White | Never Chemlight `#86B896` (a world-light colour only, ab 4.2) |

**Luminance ladder rules (art bible 4.5), stated as tests.**

- **L-1. The number is brighter than its box.** Frame ink (borders, rivets, chamfers, engraved ticks, frame labels) is never brighter than Hull Grey (L* about 61). Reading ink is Paper White (about 90) or O2 Cyan (about 79). **The stated exceptions** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the rule as written conflicted with the Paper White glyphs and keycap letters)*: the focus bracket (Tungsten, about 85), and **content ink on a frame plate**, which is not frame ink: the Paper White glyphs (warmth, tools, glowstick, carried module, notice, scanner), keycap letters and verb text. Nothing else on the frame is above 61. Alarm Red (about 47) is the mark and is distinguished by shape and the Paper White stroke inside it, not by lightness.
- **L-2. Greyscale pairs each need a shape backup.** Paper White (about 90) against Tungsten (about 85): the bracket, the bezel thickening and the position (equipped tool); the needle-mark overhang (bar). Alarm Red (about 47) against Graphite lit (about 43) against the violet core (about 43): a chamfered *filled* square with a Paper White stroke, a 1-px hairline, and an edge gleam. O2 Cyan against Paper White: a glyph against ink, never adjacent in the same role.
- **L-3. State by luminance and position, not by tint.** A reading never changes colour with its value; a zone crossing moves the needle against fixed engraving. No red numeral, no amber numeral, no flashing numeral. This is what keeps the HUD from becoming a health bar (anti-pillar: not a combat game).
- **L-4. Warmth never borrows a hue.** Cold would invite Coolant Ice and hot would invite Fuel Ember or Alarm Red; using them would assert that heat is a loop and would spend Alarm Red as decoration. The warmth dial is Paper White ink with pattern-coded zones (cold left, hot right).
- **L-5. Dead is Graphite lit.** A reading whose source is dead (no breathable source; reserve at zero) draws its glyph and strip arc band in Graphite lit while the needle and numeral stay Paper White because they are the reading, not the loop. The change is a hard cut counted once in the flash budget, not a periodic event.

**Sane-palette audit.** HUD pixels are unlit and flat, so the world's "sample Hull Grey under Tungsten" test does not apply; the audit is to sample every HUD pixel on a retained screenshot: none may fall in the Flesh band (hue 5-30 degrees, S 20-60 %, V 45-85 %) or the Violet band (hue 270-330 degrees, S > 25 %), except gleam pixels equal to `#B07BE6` during a lie. Tungsten fixture is hue 40 degrees (at least 38 required) and S 60 %, outside Flesh by hue. Pass condition at MVP: zero violet pixels anywhere on the HUD, at stage 0 and at a forced stage 3 in a stub.

### Frame geometry (visual constants the layout inherits)

| Constant | Value | Source and status |
|---|---|---|
| Plate border | 1 HUD px, Graphite lit | Proposed; "heavy" is carried by corner mass |
| Corner | 2-px chamfer with a 2 x 2 Hull Grey rivet seated in each corner | Proposed; the Foundry rhythm without the rounded corner |
| Strip outer / interior | 18 / 16 tall; at most 76.8 wide at 100 % (the cap) | ab:751; HS-11, HS-15 |
| Strip contents | `[dial arc about 24 wide x 12 tall including the pivot cap][numeral: 3 tabular digits, 5 wide, 1-px letterspace = 17][mark slot 16]` plus gaps, about 65 | Provisional on the instrument face (R-9 below) |
| Mark cell and glyph cell | 16 x 16, **live silhouette 14 x 14** | The 1-px clearance of art bible 7.2 lives *inside* the 16 x 16 cell, because the strip interior is 16 and the NEEDS_TOOL plate holds 18; amendment owed (C-G) |
| Instrument face sizes | **7-cap** (readings) and **5-cap** (labels, keycap letters, prompt verbs, captions) | ab 7.1; exactly two hand-drawn sizes (cost flag C-K) |
| Numeral | 3 tabular digits, slashed or dotted zero, distinct 1 / l / I | ab 7.1 |
| Strip dial | `STRIP_SWEEP_DEG` 180, left = low; zone bands engraved on the outer arc; edge ticks 3 px radial; **a second pre-engraved layer for the reserve source: a tank tick (3 px radial with a 2-px foot) at the full stop and a warn notch at `warn_pm`, both Hull Grey, lit only while `breath_src` is RESERVE, while the dkPa notches dim to Graphite lit (HS-12); the frame never moves, only the layer's lit-state changes** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | HS-13, HS-12 |
| Strip needle | At most 11 px (pivot on the bottom interior row) | Below the Filter's glide-eligibility floor by construction (HS-29) |
| Panel dial needle (integrity, warmth) | At least `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` (16, owned by In-World Instruments); recommended 24 if the panel can afford it | HS-16(b) |
| Panel bounding box (the drawn-content tally of D.8, not the layout) | **ROW at most 144 x 40 HUD px (22.5 % x 11.1 % of the 640 x 360 canvas) or COLUMN at most 74 x 80 (11.6 % x 22.2 %) at `H_default`, plus the notice stack above either**; the art-director's 100 x 44 box is withdrawn because the panel's content does not fit it; `/ux-design` chooses by the resulting `offered()` set *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | Must clear the 5 % safe margin and the chest keep-out |
| Prompt plate | 12 tall (ENABLED), 18 tall (NEEDS_TOOL), at most `PROMPT_PLATE_MAX_W_PX` 72 wide | Interaction ia:906 |
| Keycap | 9 x 9 (keyboard letter or pad letter at 5-cap); wide variants for Tab / Esc / Space as nine-slice | Proposed (risk C-I) |
| Bar track | Outer 6 tall (1 / 4 / 1); interior `BAR_LEN_SHORT_PX` 16 / `BAR_LEN_MEDIUM_PX` 24 / `BAR_LEN_LONG_PX` 40, outer 18 / 26 / 42 | HS-40 |

**Frame against reading at a glance (3.4).** Frame = 1-px Graphite lit hairline plus chamfer mass plus rivets, static, luminance at most 61. Reading = 1-px bright ink strokes (Paper White or O2 Cyan) that move. Stroke width is the same for both (1 HUD px, at least 3 screen px at `H >= 3`, so HR-12's 2-px floor holds by construction); the discriminating variables are luminance and motion.

### Non-Ground area allocation (`HUD_NONGROUND_PX_MAX` 2,304 HUD px at rest)

Art-director allocation, estimated from the geometry above (lit-pixel counts for 14 x 14 silhouettes are estimates; the UX spec re-measures with the drawn face).

| Surface | Budget | Estimate | Main consumers |
|---|---|---|---|
| Critical strip | 320 | about 300 | border about 150, arc band about 50, edge ticks and pivot cap about 25, needle about 11, numeral "210" about 48, rivets 16 |
| Suit panel (at most 144 x 40 as ROW or 74 x 80 as COLUMN, D.8; the area, not the box, is what is counted) | 900 | about 875 (the estimate pre-dates the ambient slot, the alarm slot and the tank-tick layer; at rest the ambient slot is blank, the alarm slot is empty and the reserve engraving is unlit, so none adds lit pixels at rest, and the UX spec re-measures) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | border about 270, four glyphs about 310, two dials about 186, reserve numeral about 48, keycap about 42, rivets 16 |
| Hand rail | 900 | about 718 | three tool glyphs about 300, three keycaps about 128, plate border about 195, glowstick and count about 75, bracket about 20; the fourth cell is reserved and empty at MVP, and the suppressant's glyph (about 100), keycap (about 42) and pips and numeral (about 26) return with the tool, X6 (estimates, re-measured by the UX spec) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| Prompt, bar, notice, mark at rest | 0 | 0 | None present at rest |
| Headroom | 184 (8 %) | about 410 unallocated at MVP while the suppressant is out (2,304 - 1,890) and about 245 once it returns (2,304 - 2,060) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the earlier "about 350" did not follow from the rows)* | Reserved for the Vertical Slice A gleam and one stray plate; the opt-in aim indicator, when enabled, draws from it (HS-52) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| **Total** | 2,304 | about 1,890 at MVP (about 2,060 once the suppressant returns) | |

Levers if the screenshot count busts the budget, in order of art cost: (1) tool glyphs at 12 x 12 live inside the 14 x 14 (loses profile detail; last); (2) rail plate border on three sides only where it abuts the safe margin; (3) the panel's reserve numeral at the row's label size; (4) rivets only on the two outer corners of each plate. The letterbox hairline the Pipeline floated (pip:86) is **declined for the HUD layer**: a 1-px perimeter hairline on a 640 x 360 canvas is 1,996 px, 87 % of the whole budget; if the Pipeline wants one it is drawn on layer 1 and excluded from the count (routed to the Pipeline owner; default off). The inspect card and every transient (prompt, bar, notice, mark, scanner reading) are outside the at-rest measurement (HS-56).

### The lie slots (built and tested at MVP, dormant until Vertical Slice A)

Applies to the five reading nodes and the scanner slot; driven by a stub Filter in tests only (HS-26). Nothing here is player-visible at MVP.

**Gleam (HS-27).** Edge-only: the glass edge of the dial arc, the needle's leading edge, the edge of the numeral window plate; never a fill, never over digits, never on the border of a non-lying reading. Colour `#B07BE6` exactly, never the core `#8A3FD1`; no alpha and no tint ramp (the palette is locked and AC-PF-57a bans Filter-sourced alpha on the HUD tree). Width 1 HUD px (`GLEAM_EDGE_PX`, PF-16 placeholder, range 1-2), at least 3 screen px at `H >= 3`. **Amplitude is encoded as lit length**: `leak_amplitude_pm` 0-1000 maps to `round(edge_len_px x amp / 1000)` whole pixels lit (never fewer than 2 while the lie is ACTIVE, which `AMP_FLOOR_PM` 400 already guarantees on any edge of 5 px or more), anchored at the needle tip or the arc's leading edge so it moves with the reading; it is position-stable and survives greyscale because it is a shape (D.13). The gleam draws only while its host reading draws; there is no `lit()` gate on the HUD. The peak-channel question (the fringe's blue channel is 230/255 = 0.90 encoded against `LEAK_MAX_CH` 0.85) is **Filter-internal** (CF-HS-12): the HUD draws the exact fringe; the Filter is asked to state the cap's unit; the layer-2 waiver is the fallback only if the unit is sRGB.

**Motion signature per reading (HR-5, art bible 7.3).**

| Reading | Host shape | Motion signature when lying | Eligible |
|---|---|---|---|
| `suit.o2` (strip) | Numeral plus short arc (needle at most 11 px) | Numeral **continuous tick or stale-hold** (PF-18); the arc carries the gleam only | Numeral yes; needle no (HS-29) |
| `suit.reserve` | Numeral | Continuous tick or stale-hold | Yes |
| `suit.integrity` | Panel dial, needle 16-24 px | **Glide** (drift plus wander, never rests) | Yes |
| `suit.warmth` | Panel dial, needle 16-24 px | **Glide**; the first VS-A lie in `READING_LIE_STAGE` order and the Player Fantasy's deepening beat | Yes |
| `suit.charge` | Cell fill, no needle | **Unresolved** (CF-HS-14); proposal: a stepped-fill substitution at `tick_phase_ms` plus the gleam on the cell outline; the art-director's alternative is fill-edge creep (the fill's top edge drifts 1 px at least every `NEVER_REST_MAX_S` around a held level), preferred because a second mark would invent a component | No, until the Filter rules |
| Scanner slot | Numerals or a zone-band pointer | Tick / stale for numerals; a band-pointer step for a band | Yes for numerals |

The honest form of each reading uses settle (arcs) or the honest grid (digits); a lying one never settles or tremors (PF-17). Tremor is not used by any suit reading (se:1203).

**Tick drawing.** A tick is a digit-glyph substitution on a tabular grid: the digit cell never reflows, the plate never inverts, no digit fades. A 7-cap digit is 5 px wide, so a substitution changes about 8-16 lit pixels out of a plate of about 3,000, well under 1 % of luminance. Cadence is the Filter's `tick_phase_ms` (D.20); the HUD only draws on it.

**Reduced motion (HR-8).** Settle becomes a one-frame step; the mark holds solid ON; glide keeps drift (scaled by `RM_GLIDE_SCALE`, floor 0.3 degrees/s) and wander; a lying tick is never faster than the Filter's published `lie_tick_min_ms` (`LIE_TICK_MS` 400 as the floor, reduced motion may raise it); no tremor substitute on the HUD.

### Event-to-visual table

Columns: **Trigger** (what the HUD reads), **Visual**, **Motion and timing** (ticks at 60 per second), **Palette**, **Class**, **Audio coupling** (names only; the HUD plays nothing). Reconciliation with the rulings: the honest grid is `HONEST_TICK_MS` 500 (pinned, tick-anchored), the settle is `HUD_SETTLE_MS` 300, and the mark clock is the beacon's `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

| HSV-1 | `perceived(suit.o2).displayed_value` changes (honest) | Numeral digit substitution on the 3-digit tabular grid | Substituted at the honest grid (`HONEST_TICK_MS`), never a tween, never a fade; may skip units across a hold | Paper White on Ground | R | None; the Suit's breath cadence is the first channel |
| HSV-2 | Same; needle target changes | Needle settles to the new position | Critically damped, monotone, no overshoot; 2 % settled at `HUD_SETTLE_MS` 300 = 18 ticks; the state `(p, v)` is kept across a retarget (D.6); the strip reads as a monotone ease-out. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | Needle Paper White | R | None |
| HSV-3 | Needle crosses a zone edge | **Nothing.** No flash, tint or pop; the engraved edge tick is simply passed | n/a | None | A | None (the breath already changed) |
| HSV-4 | `breath_src` flips between {ROOM, RESERVE} and {GASP, NONE} (HS-5, HS-20) | Strip arc band, pivot cap and the reserve and O2 glyphs: O2 Cyan to Graphite lit (or back) as a hard cut | 1 render frame, no fade; one counted source-state event, never deferred | Cyan / Graphite lit | **A** (frame register: a function of the published enum alone, bit-identical with the Filter on or off, AC-HS-139; the earlier "R-class, follows displayed state at VS-A" is withdrawn so the Filter's lie cannot leak into the frame register) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | None |

Never given a visual (per Suit, Interaction and Audio): the thrown glowstick in flight (world, Adapter); a rung change to STRAINED (no mark); any Attention, noise, lit / exposed, threat or sanity state; a room name (HS-10); a pickup or transfer toast.

### Worked examples

- **W-1. Strip O2, THIN fixture, `p_o2` 175 dkPa.** Dial 180 degrees over `O2_DIAL_MAX_DKPA` 300: needle at 105 degrees from the left stop; 11-px needle, pivot at (12, 15) in a 24 x 16 arc cell: tip (15, 4) (D.5). Zone arc lengths on radius 11 (arc length 34.6 px) at the defaults `O2_BAND_ANOXIC_PP` 100, `HYPOXIA_PP` 140 and `O2_BAND_THIN_PP` 180: ANOXIC about 11.5 px, HYPOXIC about 4.6, THIN about 4.6, NORMAL about 13.8. The numeral is `175`. No mark (STRAINED never fires one). Arc band and pivot are O2 Cyan because a breathable source exists.
- **W-2. Zone width at the `HYPOXIA_PP` range ends.** At 120 the HYPOXIC band (100-119) is about 2.3 px of arc; at 160 the THIN band (160-179) is about 2.3 px. A 2.3-px band is not legible as a pattern, so the 3-px radial edge **tick** is the primary information and band patterns are secondary (HS-13).
- **W-3. Mark timeline, reserve CRITICAL.** `RESERVE_S` 90, `RESERVE_WARN_S` 30. When the breath rung reaches CRITICAL after the `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` 600-tick hold with the reserve engaged (`breath_src` RESERVE) the marks enter **both** the strip slot (beside the breath dial, which is showing the reserve at that moment, HS-12) and the reserve-row slot (HS-22) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: each is ON if `T mod 128 < 64`, else at the next multiple of 128, in the same phase. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Pulse: ON 64 ticks (1.07 s), OFF 64 ticks. Two opposing transitions per 128 ticks make one flash, counted as one event (0.469 per second; the edge unit is retired by user ruling D91 (ii)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **W-4. Flash budget in the breach** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. STRICT mode (the first-launch default): the mark is solid, the numeral on a 500 ms grid counts 2.0/s of `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3, leaving 1.0/s for prompts. WEIGHTED mode: the numeral and the prompts are exempt by area (D.2) and the pulsing mark counts 0.469/s. With a pulsing mark beside counted numerals (a combination the two modes make rare: it arises only when the cumulative guard of D.2 returns a swap to the counted arithmetic) a 334 ms grid would put the numeral alone at 3.0/s and the total at 3.47/s, which breaks HR-13; that is why the pinned grid is 500 ms (D.2), not the Filter's 334. A lying reserve numeral at `LIE_TICK_MS` 400 (2.5/s) added in VS-A overruns in STRICT mode (2.5 + 2.0 = 4.5/s) and fits in WEIGHTED mode (2.5 + 0.469 = 2.969/s): the Filter ask, not the HUD's to override (HS-47).
- **W-5. Hold-to-confirm bar, SHORT.** `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` 0.8 s = 48 ticks, `L` 16: `fill_px = floor(progress_pm x 16 / 1000)`; 500 permille gives 8 px lit and the needle-mark at x = 8; 3 ticks per pixel; track outer 18 x 6, needle-mark 2 x 8 overhanging 1 px above and below.
- **W-6. Gleam on a lying reserve numeral window (VS-A).** The window's top edge is 17 px: `leak_amplitude_pm` 400 gives `round(17 x 400/1000)` = 7 px lit along the top edge, anchored at the end the digits tick from; 850 gives 14 px.
- **W-7. Glide visibility on the panel dial.** 24-px needle, `rate_floor = 28.6/24` = 1.19 degrees/s = 0.5 px/s of tip drift; wander 2.2 px at 0.4 Hz changes the tip pixel at least every 0.95 s. On the strip's 11-px needle the same drift would need 2.6 degrees/s, above `GLIDE_RATE_MAX_DEG` 2.0: ineligible (HS-29). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*

### Visual conflicts, risks and amendments

Kind: CONFLICT = disagrees with a locked fact or ruling; RISK = unresolved art or feasibility exposure; AMEND = an art-bible edit owed (the bible is not edited here; the amendment text is in Open Questions). Cross-References numbers in brackets.

| Id | Kind | Statement | Routed to |
|---|---|---|---|
| C-A | CONFLICT [CF-HS-9] | "Numerals tick one step per real change" (ab 7.3) cannot hold with HR-13 during decompression. The hold grid `HONEST_TICK_MS` 500 (pinned, anchored to `T`, skip-only) shows the current value at each boundary (skips allowed) and is counted in `hud_flash_budget` below the mark; 7.3's "one step" becomes "one step per grid cell, never a tween" | Art-director (7.3 wording); ruled by the user, D91 (i) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| C-B | CONFLICT, minor [CF-HS-12] | The fringe `#B07BE6` has a blue channel of 0.90 sRGB-encoded against `LEAK_MAX_CH` 0.85, but about 0.79 in linear terms. This is Filter-internal (PF-16 names both) and also applies in the world; the unit is asked first, the layer-2 waiver is the fallback | Filter, technical-artist |
| C-C | RISK | The strip budget is provisional on the instrument face (R-9): a 5-6 px digit moves the 65-px estimate by about 6 | UX spec with a drawn face |
| C-D | RISK [CF-HS-14] | `suit.charge` has no needle and no digits, so HR-5's motion channel has no host | Filter, ux-designer, art-director |
| C-E | RISK / CONFLICT, minor [CF-HS-13] | RESOLVED at CD-GDD-ALIGN: marks are held ON solid during `PAUSED_FROZEN` (the art-director's alternative, HS-23); still subject to user confirmation (OQ-HS-4) | ux-designer (the UX spec draws the held-ON mark against the pause panel) |

| C-I | RISK | A 12-px prompt plate needs a 9 x 9 keycap and a 5-cap letter (about 15 screen px at 1080p), the lowest-legibility text in the game on the primary interaction affordance; mitigation is `H` up to 2x; the accessibility tier 24 / 18 grows the plate to 15 | ux-designer, accessibility-specialist |
| C-J | RISK | `HYPOXIA_PP` range 120-160 makes a band 2.3 px wide at the extremes; ticks, not band width, carry the information | ux-designer |
| C-K | RISK | Two hand-drawn instrument-face sizes (5-cap and 7-cap), each tabular with slashed or dotted zero; ab 7.1 called several sizes "viable but costly" for a solo developer; this system needs exactly two and no more | producer (scope), art-director |
| C-L | RISK | Needles need stable sub-degree rendering on an integer grid (about 5.2 degrees per tip pixel at 11 px, about 2.4 degrees at 24 px); glide is carried by the wander, not angle resolution; the never-rest proof is spike S3 | technical-artist |
| C-M | RISK | Six marks in one phase is the loudest the HUD can be (up to 1,536 Alarm Red px); rare by construction; AC-HS-37 measures it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | ux-designer |
| C-N | RISK | A prompt plate placed beyond the subject may land in the lit cone and compete with the beam hero; acceptable by the HR-1 exemption but it must never cover the chest keep-out (16 px) | ux-designer |
| C-O | RISK | The opaque strip masks part of the cone when the chest lamp points south (accepted, ab:751); no further HUD element may enter the bottom-centre band | |
| C-P | DECLINED | `ui_mark_empty_08` (8 x 8): the default is the diagonal negative-space cut across the 16 x 16 glyph; the 8 x 8 state tick breaks 7.2 "nothing smaller" and adds an asset | consistent with HS-32 |

### Hard-requirement conformance map (art bible 7.7)

| HR | HUD requirement it creates |
|---|---|
| HR-1 | HUD in the root viewport; none of the world glow, ambient, occlusion or the Filter; a screenshot at 0 % ambient over the beam core shows text at least 4.5:1 |
| HR-2 | Every reading on an opaque Ground plate; no Hull Grey on Graphite lit |
| HR-3 | The reading set of the event table plus the reserve (C-H) |
| HR-4 | Alarm Red only on the mark; never text; mark stroke 3.8:1 |
| HR-5 | Lie slots built and dormant; C-D open |
| HR-6 | Frame, position, scale, typography and settings are anchors; nothing in the game reads or writes them |
| HR-8 | Reduced-motion rules above |
| HR-10 | No global hue remap; `pattern_strength` thickens zone patterns only |
| HR-11 | Bracket plus bezel thickening; the focus bracket cuts on the rail |
| HR-12 | 7-cap readings, 5-cap labels, stroke 1 HUD px (at least 3 screen px), text at least 4.5:1, glyphs at least 3:1 (Paper White on Ground 12.6:1; O2 Cyan 9-12:1; Hull Grey 5.3:1; Alarm Red 3.3:1 for the mark only). **Not met by Graphite lit `#5C6670` on Ground (about 2.8:1)**, which carries the lamp-OFF outline, the mark's OFF outline, the dimmed dkPa notches in RESERVE and every dead-source state; the conformance map therefore **no longer claims** the 3:1 floor for those strokes until the art-director and the accessibility-specialist either move them to Hull Grey or record a waiver (OQ-HS-83); AC-HS-145 measures every state-carrying stroke and text pixel against the floors *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* |
| HR-13 | Mark 0.469 per second (the ruled unit; the edge unit is retired); honest numerals on the 500 ms grid, exempt by area in WEIGHTED mode and counted in STRICT mode; lying digits per `T_tick`; no shake, blur or scale *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| HR-14 | The pad glyph set is the same keycap component |
| HR-15 | Not a HUD matter |

### Audio requirements (names only)

Mood the visuals must match: dry, close, mechanical; no musical sting; no stinger on a HUD event. **The HUD plays no sound.** It shows a mark when the Graph annunciator may sound (`alarm_eligible`) or a Suit rung reaches CRITICAL; the mark's leading edge is the shared clock contract (AAV-9). Sounds adjacent to HUD events, all owned elsewhere: valve click and top-up hiss (Suit), cell whine at `CELL_WARN_S`, equip clunk, lamp click, glowstick throw, the annunciator `ann`, and a UI-bus keycap tick if Audio wants one (the UI bus carries no sanity, stage or Attention effect). No HUD pulse lands on a tell beat. Under reduced motion the mark holds solid and the sound carries the alarm (AAV-19); the resulting gap is closed by the accepted `suit.alarm` mark (CF-HS-4, user ruling D89); at the first-launch default (reduce flashing ON) the mark is solid and the sound carries the alarm *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.

Stems follow art bible 8.5 (`ui_<subject>_<variant>_<size>.png`). Glyphs are authored as **1-bit white masks on transparent** and tinted by the `Theme` at draw time, so every state (Cyan, Graphite lit, Paper White, Tungsten) costs no extra asset and the palette cannot drift. The charge fill is a procedurally drawn rectangle inside the outline mask; counts are drawn images. (orchestrator ruling: where the ux-designer's and art-director's stems differ, the art-director's set is used: `ui_mark_alarm_16`, `ui_glyph_carry_16`, `ui_glyph_record_16`.) The `Theme` (`ui_theme_main`) carries the HS-20 palette (the seven loop and frame colours plus the dormant fringe `#B07BE6`; Graphite mid for the OUTCOME plate dim only), both faces and the nine-slices (art bible 7.6 hand-off).

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:hud-and-suit-layer` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions and generation prompts from this section.

## Game Feel

The HUD is a screen the player reads, not a thing they steer, so its "feel" is the quality of three promises: it answers a press instantly, it never moves unless the world did, and it never performs. Every target below is a number so a tester can pass or fail it. Ids are `GF-HS-n` (the rulings set no prefix for this section; this one is chosen so a story can cite a feel target by id). Units: ticks at `TICK_RATE` 60 (1 tick = 16.67 ms), render frames at the display rate (60 fps assumed; 30 fps is the supported floor and is noted where it matters), HUD px (one pixel of the HUD canvas = `H` screen px), and milliseconds of `present_time` (frozen under pause, HS-8). All values here are the HUD's own `[PH]` knobs or cited from their owners; none changes a simulation value.

### Feel pillars for this system (what the numbers serve)

- **Cuts, not tweens (art bible 7.3).** The only interpolations on the HUD are the dial-needle settle and the cosmetic hold-bar drain. Everything else, including every numeral change, glyph change, tool switch, mark edge, plate show and plate hide, is a hard cut within one render frame. Weight comes from mass and stillness, not from motion: the HUD never shakes, pops, flashes, pulses a reading, scales, or fades.
- **The HUD is quiet when the world is loud (P2).** In a breach the screen's own changes are bounded by `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 luminance events per rolling second; a player under pressure is never made to parse a flickering HUD (HR-13).
- **Snap means whole HUD px.** Every edge, stroke, bar cell and plate position lands on a whole HUD px at the current integer `H`; a plate follows its subject by `floor(world_to_hud(anchor_o))` from the **unjolted** camera pose, so camera jolt and hit-shake move the world and never the HUD (zero px of HUD shake, by construction).

### Responsiveness: the input-to-feedback chain

| Step | What happens | Time target | Owner of the clock | Feel rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Press | The player presses `interact` (key or pad button); the Adapter applies it as a command at the next tick | Press to command application: at most 1 tick (16.7 ms), the Controller's and kernel's | Controller / kernel | The HUD does not own this step |
| 2. ACK (pre-play) | On the **same render frame as the accepted press** the keycap face drops 1 px and the bar track appears at fill 0 (Adapter-local, never a simulation state, HS-41) | At most 1 render frame after the press (16.7 ms at 60 fps; 33 ms at 30 fps) | HUD | A press must feel received before the sim has answered |
| 3. Result | The simulation confirms (`CONFIRMED`) or rejects within `PREPLAY_MAX_TICKS` 2 (33 ms) | At most 2 ticks | Interaction | A confirm keeps the pre-play; silence withdraws it |
| 3a. Reject | The pre-play is withdrawn; the restored keycap is held Graphite-lit for `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS` 9; no shake, flash, red or text; identical for every reason *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | At most 1 render frame after the reject arrives, then 9 ticks | HUD | Rejection is quiet, not an event |
| 4. Progress | Fill advances in whole-pixel steps from `progress_pm`; a step is a position change, not a luminance event | One step per 3 ticks (SHORT, `L` 16, 48-tick hold at the default; 2.25 to 4.5 ticks across the Controller's 0.6-1.2 s range, and 6 for the Chart Table's 1.6 s Delete hold) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, 3.75 ticks (MEDIUM, `L` 24, 90-tick cap) or 6 ticks (LONG, `L` 40, 240-tick cut); a 360-tick weld on the 40-px bar is 9 ticks per step | Interaction (duration), HUD (cells) | Each step is visible; no step is held longer than 10 ticks (0.17 s) at the shipped lengths (the longest is the weld at 9 ticks, 0.15 s), which is why `BAR_LEN_LONG_PX` has a lower tuning bound |
| 5a. Done | The bar holds full for `BAR_HOLD_DONE_TICKS` 12 (200 ms), then cuts. No checkmark, flash or text | 12 ticks | HUD | Completion is felt by the world changing, not the HUD celebrating |
| 5b. Abort (`ended_by` `WORLD`) | Fill snaps empty; a 2 x 2 Ground break-notch is held `BAR_HOLD_DONE_TICKS` 12 (200 ms), then the bar withdraws; the same frame set for every world cause *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | 12 ticks | HUD | Breaking is seen once and not explained |
| 5c. Player cancel (a channel `PLAYER_CANCEL`; a hold-to-confirm release is the Controller's `b`) | The fill drains right to left over `BAR_DRAIN_MS` 400 from any fill and any length class (D.16); a hold release follows the Controller's `b` (0.4 s from full on SHORT); reduced motion snaps to 0 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the row kept the retired drain scaled by `CONFIRM_DRAIN_RATE` times the fill speed)* | 0.4 s for every length class | HUD (cosmetic) | A channel cancel is keyed off `ended_by` = `PLAYER_CANCEL` (CF-HS-17, answered); the hold-to-confirm release stays provisional on the Controller's hold read (CF-HS-16) |
| 5d. Outcome | The bar fills as DONE and is removed at once; the plate border steps Graphite lit to Graphite mid for 12 ticks; no text | 12 ticks | HUD | The world's cue (the seam, the spark) is the message |
| 6. Hide | A plate hides on the same render frame `prompt_state` goes HIDDEN or `focus_ref` changes; the bar outlives it only until the simulation's abort (at most 5 dark ticks) | 0 frames of delay | HUD | Hide is never debounced, never deferred |

**Sim-to-screen latency of a reading.** The HUD pulls its five `perceived` records every render frame (HS-7) and is last in the presenter order (camera solver, Avatar Presenter, Filter, HUD, Lighting). A value committed at tick `T` is therefore drawn on the first render frame after the commit: worst case 1 tick (16.7 ms) plus 1 render frame (16.7 ms) = 33 ms at 60 fps. The numeral then additionally waits for the next grid boundary (below); the needle starts its settle on that same frame.

### Timing: the numbers a tester can measure

| Id | Behaviour | Value | Source and range | Reduced motion / reduce flashing |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GF-HS-1 | Dial-needle settle (integrity, warmth, strip O2) | `HUD_SETTLE_MS` 300 = 18 ticks (range 250-400); critically damped, monotone, no overshoot; exactly 2 % of the step remains at 300 ms (0.019999) and 1.5 % at frame 19; on a mid-settle retarget the position and velocity are kept; steady lag under a -12 dkPa/s ramp is about 1.2 dkPa (0.74 degrees); no backward motion in a one-direction run *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | D.6 | One-frame step (`p = g` on the retarget) |
| GF-HS-2 | Visible overshoot | None by construction (the response is monotone); the earlier 0.2 px / 0.4 px estimate is withdrawn *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | D.6 | n/a |
| GF-HS-3 | Honest numeral change (O2, reserve) | A cut on the shared grid: `HONEST_TICK_MS` 500 = 30 ticks (pinned; `T mod 30 == 0`, skip-only); at most 2 per second per numeral; shows the then-latest value; never queued, tweened or faded | D.2, HS-14 | Unchanged (a cut already) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| GF-HS-4 | Numeral lag behind the truth | At most one grid cell (500 ms) in steady state; at most `2 x HONEST_TICK_MS` = 1000 ms when a boundary is skipped for a waiting prompt, **unconditionally** (a boundary is never skipped twice in a row, so a hue cut plus two prompts cannot push it to 1500 ms) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | D.2 grid-skip rule | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-5 | Reserve top-up | Counts upward in whole-second steps at the grid; never a tween | HS-17 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-6 | Warning-mark period | `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128 ticks = 2.133 s (range 120-180 ticks = 2.0-3.0 s); duty `MARK_DUTY_PM` 500 = 64 ticks ON (1.067 s) and 64 OFF; hard cut both edges | D.4, HS-23 | Solid ON, no edges |
| GF-HS-7 | Mark appearance latency | At most 64 ticks (1.07 s) when the rung arrives just after the ON phase; 0 when it arrives inside the ON phase; hide is immediate (0 frames) | D.4 | Immediate (solid) |
| GF-HS-8 | Mark luminance events | 0.469 per second (the ruled unit: one pair of edges is one flash); all marks share one phase, so six marks are the same events as one | D.2 | 0 (solid; the first-launch default) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| GF-HS-9 | Prompt show debounce | `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS` 13 = 217 ms of stable focus before a plate appears (range 13-20 ticks, 217-333 ms); an accepted press shows the plate on that frame *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | D.18 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-10 | Prompt cycle floor | `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` 20 = 333 ms between show-starts; at most 3 show-starts per second; floor `ceil_div(TICK_RATE, FLASH_MAX_PER_S)` = 20 | D.2, D.18 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-11 | Prompt show / hide | Show: a cut after the debounce. Hide: a cut on the same render frame | HS-37 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-12 | Lamp glyph | The beam toggles on the tick; the charge-row outline follows at once unless latched; `GLYPH_STATE_LATCH_TICKS` 20 (333 ms, range 20-60) caps the whole state-display class (glyph, bracket, count, TAP relabel) at 3 drawn changes per second and the glyph may lag the beam by 0.33 s in the common case and at most `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 60 (1 s) when the window is full *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the world beam is never debounced | HS-19, D.2 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-13 | Tool switch (rail) | Tungsten fixture and bracket move by a hard cut on the render frame the equip is accepted | HSV-13 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-14 | Glowstick count | Steps on the tick the throw is accepted, not when the stick lands | HS-34 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-15 | Device glyph swap | At most 1 render frame; at most one swap per `DEVICE_SWITCH_HYSTERESIS` (250 ms, cited); latched while a channel is non-IDLE; no popup | HS-35 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-16 | Failed-write notice | A cut in, persistent, no animation; cleared by a cut on the next `WRITTEN` | HS-46 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-17 | First-frame line (UI-7; also after a `DEATH_STUB` restore, HS-46, user ruling D15 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*) | A cut in on the first `RUNNING` frame, held `UI7_DWELL_S` 8 s on `present_time`, a cut out | HS-46 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-18 | Scanner reading | A cut in when the result lands, held `SCAN_READING_DWELL_S` 6 s, a cut out | HS-33 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-19 | Time step clamp | The first step after a pause, focus loss or hitch is clamped to `HUD_DT_CLAMP_MS` 50; no latch, debounce, settle or tick fires from the gap | HS-8 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-20 | Pause / resume | Pause freezes every HUD timer with the HUD undimmed (no dim, blur or desaturation) and holds an active mark ON solid, the one exception (HS-23); resume applies the clamped step and a forced discrete rebuild | HS-53 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-21 | Zoom or `H` change mid-channel | A cut: on an `H` change the layout and plate re-project on the next render frame; on a zoom step only the plate re-projects (no layout change, no SCALE_CHANGE); the bar is re-laid from `progress_pm`; no animation between scales *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | HS-9, EC-HS-1 | Unchanged |
| GF-HS-22 | Restore | The first `RUNNING` frame draws restored values directly: no settle, no abort frame, no notch | HS-53 | Unchanged |

### Weight and snap

- **Weight.** A frame is heavy by mass and still; a reading is light by thin bright strokes and motion (art bible 3.4). No HUD element has easing except the needle settle and the cosmetic drain; there is no ease-in, ease-out, bounce, slide, wipe, scale-pop or fade anywhere. A needle never overshoots (the settle is monotone, D.6). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **Snap.** Placement is integer HUD px at every `H` (D.9): at the Deck's 1280 x 800 with `H` 3 over `k` 2 the canvas is 426.67 x 240 HUD px, every rectangle is floored to a whole HUD px and the 2-screen-px remainder is split evenly as margin. The bar fills in whole cells (`floor(progress_pm x L / 1000)`), never a partial pixel. A needle's tip lands on a whole HUD px (35 / 51 / 76 pre-baked frames at `L` 11 / 16 / 24 at the 180-degree sweep, D.15; 68 and 101 are the 240-degree counts for `L` 16 and 24, a formula case only) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **Input can make the HUD no louder than the ceiling through its state displays, and every acknowledgement is bounded and recorded** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the earlier sentence promised that no input raises the HUD above the ceiling, which HS-47's counted-never-deferred events falsified)*. Spamming `interact` is bounded by `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS`; cycling tools at 10 Hz, throwing sticks, toggling the lamp ten times a second and tapping the TAP-mode confirm all pass the one shared state-display limiter (at most one drawn change per `GLYPH_STATE_LATCH_TICKS` 20 across the class, so at most 3 a second, each showing the latest state); no deferrable event can exceed `FLASH_MAX_PER_S`; the acknowledgement cues (ACK dip with its force-admitted plate, reject hold, OUTCOME border) are counted, rate-gated and may overrun a full window, and an overrun is recorded as `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN`, never hidden (AC-HS-72, AC-HS-140).
- **Stillness at rest.** With all readings OK, no prompt, no mark and no notice, no HUD pixel changes from one render frame to the next except a numeral substitution on a real change; the at-rest picture is a held breath (Player Fantasy: held composure).

### The breach second by second (the reference feel scenario)

A door breach with the player holding the welder, `p_o2` falling 10 to 15 dkPa per second through the 10 to 30 s tell:

1. Seconds before CRITICAL: the O2 numeral changes at most twice a second on its 500 ms grid and the strip needle settles over 300 ms each time; no mark, no colour change, no sound from the HUD (P2: the world tells first). When the valve clicks (`breath_src` becomes RESERVE) the strip's dial swaps to the reserve in one counted source-state event (HS-12, HSV-42): the numeral then counts whole seconds down on the same grid, the needle settles to the reserve scale, and the ambient numeral continues in the panel's ambient slot; the step-1 grid arithmetic is unchanged because both numerals share the one grid. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
2. At the Suit's CRITICAL plus the `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` 600-tick hold: the strip mark slot starts its pulse at the next 128-tick boundary (at most 64 ticks, 1.07 s); thereafter 1.067 s ON, 1.067 s OFF as a hard cut, the numeral still on its grid (**at the first-launch default, reduce flashing ON, the mark is solid from its first frame and there is no pulse**). Luminance events: the pulsing mark is 0.469 per second under the ruled unit; WEIGHTED mode (reduce flashing off) counts only that, the numeral (2.0 per second) and the prompts being exempt by area; STRICT mode counts the numeral at 2.0 per second with the mark solid, leaving 1.0 per second for prompt show-starts *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
3. In STRICT mode a prompt that would exceed the window waits one grid cell; the numeral's boundary is skipped and shows the then-latest value one boundary later (lag at most 1000 ms, unconditionally). **Priority confirmed in this scenario (stage 12):** with the numeral falling on every boundary (2 events/s), the **typical** stable-focus-to-plate time is the debounce alone, 13 ticks (217 ms), because the prompt is the third event in the window and `admit` passes it; **when a further counted event is already in the window** (named in this scenario: the one-event breath-source swap at the valve click, HS-12, the ACK of an earlier press, or the orphan hide of the previous plate) the window can hold 3 of `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3, a prompt show-start is the only deferrable event that outranks the grid boundary, so it takes the room by displacing the next grid boundary (grid-skip, never twice in a row), and the worst-case time from a stable focus to the plate (no press yet) is the debounce plus one grid cell, 13 + 30 = 43 ticks (717 ms) in this example, and at most 13 + `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 60 = 73 ticks when several such events stack; in WEIGHTED mode the plate shows after the debounce alone, 13 ticks, 217 ms; a press force-admits the plate at once, only for an ENABLED or NEEDS_TOOL subject (HS-37) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The numeral pays (a skipped boundary, lag at most 1000 ms), the prompt does not; the honest grid never outranks the player's next action. Whether this is ever perceived as late is OQ-HS-59.
4. The player stops the weld mid-channel (`ended_by` = `PLAYER_CANCEL`): the fill drains right to left over `BAR_DRAIN_MS` 400 (reduced motion: snaps to 0), then the bar withdraws; a world abort (`ended_by` = `WORLD`, for example the subject going dark) instead snaps the fill empty and holds the 2 x 2 notch 200 ms (HS-41, D.16); the mark and numeral are unaffected. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the step described the snap-plus-notch for a player stop, which D.16 and AC-HS-65 draw as a drain)*

### Feel acceptance hooks

Each target above is asserted either as a pure-function test (GF-HS-1 to GF-HS-8, GF-HS-10, GF-HS-12 by AC-HS-20, AC-HS-32 to AC-HS-35, AC-HS-55, AC-HS-74), a stub-driven integration test (the chain: AC-HS-61 to AC-HS-66), or a retained screenshot with lead sign-off (the settle's look, the mark pulse, glyph legibility at 16 x 16: AC-HS-36, AC-HS-37). The feel qualities that cannot be automated (whether the settle "reads" as a calm instrument, whether the mark is alarming without being frantic) are signed off by the creative-director and art-director on the retained captures and re-checked in the first playtest (Open Questions). **Competence hook (stage 12):** in the reference breach scenario the prompt show-start latency from a stable focus to the plate (no press yet) is **13 ticks (217 ms) typically** and at most 43 ticks (717 ms) in STRICT mode (the first-launch default) when a third counted event is in the window (73 ticks at the `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` bound), and 13 ticks (217 ms) in WEIGHTED mode, and 0 frames after an accepted press on an ENABLED or NEEDS_TOOL subject *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, with the prompt outranking the honest grid (AC-HS-127, AC-HS-144); and whether a breach-time valve prompt is ever perceived as late is asked in the first playtest (OQ-HS-59), with the fallback named there.

## UI Requirements

This system **is** a UI: it owns the always-on, in-play screen layer (layer 2 of the Pipeline, in the root viewport) and the shared component set that other screens reuse. It owns no menu, no settings screen and no inspect-card content. The sections below list what the UX specs must cover, what the HUD hands to neighbours, and what is explicitly not here. Stories for HUD surfaces cite the UX spec, not this GDD (R42).

### Surfaces this system owns

| Surface | Anchor and size constraint | Contents | Source read | Register | State coverage the UX spec must draw |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `hud_strip` | Centre-bottom on the bottom 5 % safe margin; at most 12 % x 5 % of the picture at 100 % scale at the 1080p reference (76 x 18 HUD px); `strip_budget` D.1 | `[breath dial arc with pivot cap][numeral][mark slot]` (ambient O2 in ROOM, GASP and NONE; the reserve in RESERVE, HS-12) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `perceived(suit.o2)`; `perceived(suit.reserve)` while RESERVE; `breath_src` and the Suit's rung via `view` | Reading plus frame | Rest; THIN; HYPOXIC; ANOXIC (needle on the left stop, numeral 0, band Graphite lit); enriched (clamp at 999); **RESERVE: full (90), mid, at the warn notch, empty (0), with the tank tick and warn notch lit and the dkPa notches Graphite, in colour and in greyscale (AC-HS-137)**; mark ON, OFF, SOLID in ROOM and in RESERVE; no breathable source; DEAD frozen; honest numeral mid-grid |
| `hud_suit_panel` | Bottom-left inside the safe margin; layout is the UX spec's (CF-HS-18) under the invariants of HS-16 | Reserve row (O2 ring-clamp glyph, whole-seconds numeral, the ambient-O2 slot lit only while the strip shows the reserve), integrity dial, warmth dial, charge cell with the lamp-switch outline and `toggle_beam` keycap, a mark slot per reading, the ship-alarm mark slot (user ruling D89; position the UX spec's), the notice slot, the reserved `own_identity` slot *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | `perceived` x 4, `view` | Reading plus frame | Reserve full, mid, low, empty, engaged, topped up; integrity at each zone tick; warmth in each of the five load zones (and at the nominal reading with `WZ_COMFORT` lit); charge full, low, `CELL_WARN_S`, `CELL_ENABLED` 0 (empty slot); lamp on and off; each mark slot ON and OFF, the ship-alarm slot ON and OFF; the failed-write notice and first-frame line stacked at the edge |
| `hud_rail` | Bottom-right inside the safe margin; may be covered by a co-op OVERLAY panel | Three tool slots (cutter, welder, scanner) in a four-slot geometry, with keycaps `1..3` or the pad pair `LB` / `RB`, equipped bracket, glowstick glyph and count (the suppressant's slot, pips and numeral are dormant until the tool returns, user ruling X6 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*), carried-module glyph with the drop keycap, scanner reading slot | `view`, tool reads, the glyph token read, `perceived(SCANNER)` | Frame plus one reading slot | Each tool equipped; the fourth cell empty; glowstick 4 to 0; carrying a module; scanner reading present and absent; keyboard and each pad glyph set; rail hidden by an overlay |
| `hud_prompt` | `world_to_hud(anchor_o)` of the focused subject, with the AD-5 side order, the chest keep-out and the margin | Plate with one keycap and one verb (12 tall); NEEDS_TOOL variant (18 tall, notched, 16 x 16 tool glyph); anchor tab | `focus_view(seat)` | Meta | Each verb token; a two-line wrap; a +40 % pseudo-locale string; the wide `?` unmapped keycap; carry-state prompts (`G . Drop`, install, pod-console hop, the door face's V4 plate *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*); placement fallbacks; withheld |
| `hud_bar` | On the prompt plate's long edge facing away from the anchor | Linear track at `BAR_LEN_SHORT_PX` 16 / `BAR_LEN_MEDIUM_PX` 24 / `BAR_LEN_LONG_PX` 40, Tungsten fill, Paper White needle-mark | `channel_view(seat)` | Meta | Pre-play ACK, CONFIRMED, PROGRESS at several fills, hold-to-confirm drain, ABORT with break-notch, DONE, OUTCOME dim, reject |
| `hud_give_up_plate` *(exists only while `confirm_view.give_up_live`, HS-42(b))* | Around the projected chest point, outside the 16-px keep-out (HS-39), above / below / right / left order, never withheld | Plate with one keycap, the verb "Give up" (`plate_give_up`), SHORT linear bar with needle-mark | `confirm_view(seat)` incl. `give_up_live`, `glyph_view(seat, give_up)` | Meta | Hidden; shown at fill 0; hold at 25 / 50 / 100 %; release drain; TAP armed ("Confirm?", thick bracket); both glyph sets (KBM Backspace, PAD View); placement fallbacks (below / right / left); pseudo-locale +40 % *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `hud_notice` | At the suit panel's edge | Failed-write notice; first-frame line | `save_status`, the boot flag | Meta | Notice alone; line alone; both stacked |
| `hud_aim_indicator` *(conditional; exists only while `aim_indicator` is on, user ruling D9)* | At a fixed radius from the projected chest point, outside the chest keep-out | One direction mark (HS-52) | the Controller's aim direction | Meta | Off (no node); on at each of the 16 aim steps with the lamp on and off *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| Inspect-card frame | Layer 2, placement and content by In-World Instruments | The HUD supplies the frame, `Theme` and components only | Instruments | Frame HUD-owned; reading Instruments' | The shared component set only; the card itself is the Instruments UX spec's *(designed)* |
| Reserved anchors | `crew_strip` top-left, `chat` left edge below it, `caption` plate slot, `own_identity` on the panel | Nothing at MVP | none | Anchor | Empty; the layout leaves room (ab:889: "designed now so the single-player HUD leaves room") |

### Shared component set and `Theme` (lands first, `design/ux/interaction-patterns.md`)

One Godot `Theme` (`ui_theme_main`) and the shared components: keycap (keyboard, Xbox, PlayStation, wide `?`), Ground plate (nine-slice, chamfered, rivet corners), chamfered bezel, focus bracket, linear bar with needle-mark, tabular-numeral label, gleam-on-glass helper, and the held-stress-mark component Instruments uses. The HUD's UX spec lands first and the Chart Table cites the set (a Soft lateral dependency); the components are the look only, and behaviour stays with each owner (HS-42, CT-22, Chart Table C19). No node carries a per-node colour, font or stylebox override (AC-HS-108).

### Settings hooks the HUD consumes (screens owned elsewhere)

`ui_scale_step` (the only route to `H`; labelled by cap height 21 / 28 / 35 / 42 screen px; offered only at steps where `bottom_row_fit` holds), `reading_face_scale` (21 / 15 px tier by default, 24 / 18 as the accessibility tier), `reduced_motion`, `reduce_flashing` (ON at first launch, user ruling D79), `aim_indicator` (off, user ruling D9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, `hud_opacity` (100 %, hidden at MVP, CF-HS-8), `pattern_strength` (0 to 3, thickens zone patterns only), `captions` (a Ground plate slot, Adaptive Audio's AAV-38), plus the Filter's three published scalars consumed not owned. All are anchors (HR-6): no game state reads or writes them. The settings screens, the tier switch and the opacity floor belong to Accessibility Settings and Settings & Input Rebinding (no file on disk; provisional).

### Surfaces owned elsewhere (this system provides a seam, not the screen)

| Surface | Owner | The seam |
|---|---|---|
| Chart table, web map, main menu, Continue, pause menu | Chart Table, Web Map & Menus (designed) | The HUD stays visible and frozen behind it in solo pause; shares the component set; hands over the UI-7 line (the HUD shows nothing if the Chart Table showed it) |
| Inspect card content, in-world gauges | In-World Instruments & Inspect View (designed) | Frame, `Theme`, components and the gleam helper from the HUD; the card numeral rides the honest grid and requests slots from `hud_flash_budget` |
| Debug overlay | Debug Overlay (True Values) (designed) | Layer 4, dev builds only; the HUD exposes `hud_debug_snapshot()`, a read-only channel the Overlay consumes (do:417, do:777: `H`, picture rect, plate rectangles, mark phase, last model and the nine AC-HS-122 counters; no `k`), and hosts nothing |
| Crew strip, chat, pings, co-op overlay | Crew Strip HUD, Chat/Voice/Pings, Co-op Session (undesigned, Vertical Slice B) | Reserved `HudLayout` anchors only |
| Accessibility and rebinding screens | Accessibility Settings; Settings & Input Rebinding (undesigned) | The hooks above |
| Onboarding | Onboarding (Cryo Bay) (undesigned) | No overlay, hint or tooltip at MVP; a future first-use prompt uses the existing plate |

### Input and focus rules for the UX spec

- Keyboard and pad parity (T6): every core-loop HUD flow is gamepad-complete (rail cycle `LB` / `RB`, drop, `toggle_beam`, hold-to-confirm, focus bracket on navigation); text chat is the only keyboard-only item.
- In `gameplay` every HUD node is non-focusable and ignores the mouse; gamepad focus is never assigned to a HUD node; no OS cursor is shown (the chest beam is the pointer; the opt-in aim indicator of HS-52 is a node, not a cursor, and takes no input) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. In `chart_table` and `chat` the cursor is shown; in `inspect_card` it stays hidden (inert) because `set_aim` stays live (HS-52).
- The glyph set follows the Adapter's glyph device (never `InputEvent.device`, never `device == 0`); swaps are latched while a channel is non-IDLE.
- Plate text is localised through `tr()` StringName keys; the face is Latin-only by construction and other scripts use the reading face with measured geometry.

### Items carried to the UX specs (decisions this GDD deliberately leaves open)

1. **Panel geometry** (CF-HS-18): the three specialists differ (about 129 x 38 with 18-px cells; at most 100 x 44; a 4-row column); the UX spec chooses under HS-16 and must keep every needle-only reading at least `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` long, inside the budget of the arrangement it chooses (ROW 144 x 40 or COLUMN 74 x 80; the 100 x 44 box is withdrawn, D.8; the choice is by the resulting `offered()` set with the accessibility-specialist's sign-off, OQ-HS-81) and with `PANEL_SWEEP_DEG` 180 (the only drawn sweep); it also draws the ambient-O2 slot, the strip's reserve engraving and the notice stack (**required deliverable, not optional**) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **A drawn feasibility sketch (HS-54) precedes the UX spec.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
2. **The bottom-row fit option** (CF-HS-11): option (a) is the adopted MVP stopgap (the settings offer exactly `offered(P_w, P_h)`, D.8); (b) a deterministic stacked layout under a width breakpoint is evaluated before Alpha; (c) slim variants stay open. The sizing constraint is **100 HUD px per side** (panel and rail each), because the strip is centred (the earlier 204 px for the pair is withdrawn); `H_default` and `H_default + 1` are offered on every picture in the D.8 table. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
3. **The drawn instrument face** (R-9): all strip and panel widths are provisional until the 5-cap and 7-cap faces exist.
4. **Keycap and plate height** (C-I): 12-px plates with a 5-cap letter are the lowest-legibility text on the primary affordance; the 24 / 18 tier grows the plate to 15.
5. **Warmth axis and edges**: closed. The Suit publishes the axis and the four inner edges (SE-29; five load zones, COMFORT at 500); the UX spec draws five zones and four notches from D.14, with the notch tick as the information. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
6. **Interaction's open questions 22 and 23** (plate placement offset, carry-prompt shape), carried here.
7. **Plate overflow**: grow, never clip (HS-49); the UX spec sizes the grown plate and the exclusion region.
8. **The charge-cell lie motion signature** (CF-HS-14, dormant): stepped-fill substitution or fill-edge creep.
9. **The hold, tap and tap-twice grammar (REQUIRED deliverable; re-review)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: it was an optional "first-use affordance", yet the three keycap variants are indistinguishable at a glance as written)*: the UX spec draws the keycap variants for HOLD (a 1-px bar under the cap), TAP (two pips) and the armed TAP state (relabelled plate plus a thickened bracket) so that each differs from the others by **shape at 16 screen px of keycap and in greyscale**, with a pass check (three captures, three distinct silhouettes); and, if playtest shows the hold needs a first-use hint, only the existing plate is used (no tutorial overlay).
10. **Lamp-off aim reference for mouse players (REQUIRED; OQ-HS-63; HS-52).** With the lamp off there is no beam, and the OS cursor is hidden in `gameplay`; the UX spec must specify how a mouse player still reads where they are aiming. Constraints: (a) the cue is **in the world picture, not on the HUD**: the Controller's **16-step chest-plate swivel** (authored as 16 pre-drawn steps, HS-52; PR-4 rule 5 forbids a runtime rotation) carries the aim, with the avatar's facing and hand-pose (the five facings) as the coarse read only, because a five-facing pose is too coarse for a throw on its own *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: this item named the five-facing hand pose as the carrier, which HS-52 had already replaced)*; (b) it is **never a HUD cursor, reticle, crosshair or aim line** (the anti-combat guard rail; HS-DT-2: the HUD draws no ship or world state); (c) it adds **no light, glow or contrast** to the dark (P3 and the lamp-off strategy), so it cannot be a lit marker, and it must be readable at the darkest ambient in which the avatar sprite is drawn; (d) it holds at every integer zoom step (it is world art and scales with the world), **including the minimum and the maximum step, where the pixel separation between adjacent swivel steps is measured and recorded** (AC-HS-126). The UX spec may need an ASK to the Controller (facing quantisation) and the Avatar Presenter (self-visibility with the lamp off, the 16 step frames); neither is changed here. Verified by AC-HS-126 (a vertical-slice go / no-go; the outcome and the owners are in HS-52). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
11. **The opt-in aim indicator (user ruling D9; HS-52, OQ-HS-78):** drawn form, radius and 16-step behaviour, inside the invariants of HS-52 (direction only, class M, no light, Paper White, outside the chest keep-out, no input); it is off by default and does not replace item 10 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
12. **The ship-alarm mark slot (user ruling D89; HS-22, HS-25):** its position on the suit panel inside the chosen arrangement's budget (D.8), and its inclusion in the feasibility sketch of HS-54 (OQ-HS-33) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.

### Accessibility notes (a project-wide gap)

`design/accessibility-requirements.md` does not exist and is named as a gap (owner: the accessibility-specialist, who authors it later with `/ux-design accessibility` and records the `H` ceiling of D.8 / OQ-HS-34 there (133 % under COLUMN, 100 % under ROW on a `k_auto` = 2 display); the `suit.alarm` entry it would have needed is moot, the user having accepted the mark); the project's accessibility tier is **standard** (D80, default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The HUD's contributions to it are: opaque Ground plates (HR-2), text at least 4.5:1 and glyphs at least 3:1 (HR-12), every colour with a shape backup (4.7), no hue-only state, reduced motion and reduce flashing honoured on the mark, the settle and glide, the 3-per-second ceiling (HR-13), pad parity, the UI scale hook by integer step, and the closed gap that a deaf player with reduced motion has a HUD alarm cue, the accepted `suit.alarm` mark (CF-HS-4, user ruling D89, so the P2 tell does not depend on audio); reduce flashing defaults ON (D79), the two-tap alternative and the adjustable hold (D80), and the opt-in aim indicator (D9) are the HUD's accessibility hooks. **Physical size on the Steam Deck (RM-DECK, Steam Deck LCD 2022, the handheld reference machine of delegated ruling R12) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)***: the 5-cap text and the 7-cap numerals are 5 and 7 art px at `H` 3, so 15 and 21 screen px; their physical cap heights are those pixel counts times the panel's pixel pitch, which this GDD does not state, because the pitch is `[UNV]` in the pinned reference; the UX spec or the accessibility-specialist **records the measured cap heights in millimetres** on RM-DECK, and an HR-12 legibility judgement for the 5-cap keycap letters on the Deck waits on that figure (OQ-HS-36).

📌 **UX Flag — HUD & Suit Layer**: this system has real UI. In Pre-Production, run `/ux-design` to create the UX specs for `design/ux/hud.md` (primary: the strip, panel, rail, mark, prompt plate, bar and notices) and `design/ux/interaction-patterns.md` (the shared component set and `Theme`, which lands first) **before writing epics**. `design/accessibility-requirements.md` does not exist and is named as a gap. Stories that reference HUD UI should cite `design/ux/hud.md`, not this GDD.

This section declares every explicit dependency on another document's specific rule, value or name, in four tables: (1) the contracts that neighbouring GDDs and the art bible have written *about* the HUD, each answered **accept**, **answer** (accepted with a decision) or **push back**, indexed K1 to K43 for the review commands *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; (2) the references this GDD makes in the template's four-column form; (3) the locked constants and registry names used, with their owners; (4) the conflicts surfaced. Every row quoted in Interactions with Other Systems was re-read from its file at the line given there. The line numbers in table 1 are cited from the stage brief and drift between runs (for example the Audio rows read at aa:178, 560 and 1243 on re-read, not the 177, 555 and 1233 listed in K37); the confirming consistency check re-reads them. The registry is cited by entry name because its line numbers drift.
