### 4. Conflicts surfaced (none resolved silently; none changes a locked value)

| Id | Conflict | Parties | Disposition in this GDD |
|---|---|---|---|
| CF-HS-1 | The Suit's access-path clause says readings reach the HUD only through `true_value`; the registry and the Filter say `perceived` | Suit SE-29 vs Filter / registry | `perceived` adopted (HS-4); Suit refresh owed |
| CF-HS-2 | Ship Body expects a room-and-compartment readout from the HUD | Ship Body vs art bible 7.0 and Graph UI item 4 | Rejected (HS-10); Body refresh owed |
| CF-HS-3 | HR-12 "every critical needle carries a numeral" vs the Suit's ban on a health number and a body-temperature number | art bible HR-12 vs Suit anti-requirements | HR-12 read as the strip's O2 needle only (HS-18); wording amendment routed to the art-director |
| CF-HS-4 | The art bible fires the mark on "a ship alarm the suit receives"; the Suit has no read for it; a reduced-motion deaf player has no HUD alarm cue | art bible 7.2, 7.4 / HR-8 vs Suit read set vs Audio AAV-19 | **Closed by user ruling D89 / D43:** `suit.alarm` is accepted at MVP (HS-25) and the mark count is six *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| CF-HS-5 | The Suit places the mark "beside the O2 glyph"; the strip has no O2 glyph (width) | Suit SE-30 vs the 12 % x 5 % cap | No strip glyph (HS-11); wording refresh owed |
| CF-HS-6 | The strip's arc cannot carry the glide (an 11-px needle is below the 14.3-px eligibility floor) | Filter PF-17 vs the strip cap | Lie rides the numeral plus the gleam; glide on a panel dial (HS-29); Filter asked to state PF-17 for HUD dials |
| CF-HS-7 | The Pipeline says layers 2-4 run on process time and also that the HUD is frozen under pause | Pipeline PR-9 vs its own state table | Layer 2 on `present_time` (HS-8); PR-9 prose refresh owed |
| CF-HS-8 | A HUD opacity setting (70 % floor) vs HR-2's opaque Ground plates | art bible 7.4 vs HR-2 | No opacity setting at MVP; a hook at 100 % (HS-50) |
| CF-HS-9 | **Honest numerals vs HR-13:** "numerals tick one step per real change" (art bible 7.3) cannot hold with a venting rate above 3 dkPa/s and the 3-per-second ceiling | art bible 7.3 vs HR-13 / PR-13 / AC-PF-42 | A shared hold grid `HONEST_TICK_MS` (HS-14): **amends locked art bible text**; the amendment is a user ruling (D91 (i)) and the art-director applies it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| CF-HS-10 | **The flash unit is undefined across the repo:** the Filter counts digit substitutions, Interaction counts prompt cycles, the mark counts at its leading edge; at Vertical Slice A a lying digit alone fills the ceiling | Filter PF-18, Interaction D.UI-2, Pipeline PR-13, the HUD | **Closed by user ruling D91 (ii):** a flash is a pair of opposing transitions counted `ceil(n_transitions / 2)`; the arbiter's leading-edge count bounds it from above (D.2); the edge unit is retired *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
| CF-HS-11 | **The bottom row does not fit at the top of the `H` range on `k_auto` = 2 displays** (53 HUD px per side at `H` 6 on a 1280-px picture with the strip plate at its 76-px cap, against a 100-px budget; the earlier sum figures 107 / 118 are withdrawn) vs "3x-6x" and "100-200 %" | Pipeline PR-12 and art bible HR-12 vs the layout | **MVP stopgap adopted (design review 2026-10-27): option (a), the settings offer only `offered(P_w, P_h)` (D.8, HS-54); the `H` ceiling on `k_auto` 2 displays and the Deck (133 % under COLUMN, 100 % under ROW, D.8) is an accessibility cost with an owner (OQ-HS-34, OQ-HS-81); option (b) is evaluated before Alpha** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| CF-HS-12 | **Filter-internal:** PF-16 (pf:312) names both the fringe `#B07BE6` and `LEAK_MAX_CH` 0.85, and the fringe's blue channel is 230/255 = 0.90 sRGB-encoded (fails) but about 0.79 linear (passes); the cap's rationale (glow bloom, processed before tonemapping) is a linear-space concern; the conflict applies to world gleams as well as the HUD | Filter PF-16 against itself and the locked palette | No value changed; the exact fringe is drawn (HS-27). **ASK 1 (Filter):** state the cap's unit. **ASK 2, only if the unit is sRGB:** waive the cap for layer 2 (HUD glass and the inspect-card glass drawn through the HUD helper), re-tested if HDR output is ever enabled on the root |
| CF-HS-13 | A frozen OFF phase under pause can read as "safe" on a CRITICAL reading | art-director vs "last frame held" | **Resolved (stage 12):** any active mark is held ON solid while `PAUSED_FROZEN` (HS-23), the one stated exception to "last frame held"; confirmed 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20 (OQ-HS-4) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20)* |
| CF-HS-14 | The charge cell is a fill, neither a dial nor a numeral: HR-5 has no motion signature for it | HR-5 vs PF-17, PF-18 | Dormant at MVP; proposal and owners in Open Questions |
| CF-HS-15 | A lying suit reading can lie its rung mark away | Suit UI-3 vs P4 vs Sanity SN-19 (see CF-HS-19) | Accepted and stated (HS-30, split by source); the body is the remaining channel |
| CF-HS-16 | `glyph_for(action, device)`, the hold fraction and the pressed action were not published by any GDD | this GDD vs Controller / Interaction | **Largely closed by the neighbours' own design reviews (2026-10-27):** the Controller publishes `confirm_view = {b, pressed, released}` and `glyph_view = {device, token}` (pci:953, closed `keycap_token` enumeration) and has `confirm_mode` {HOLD, TAP}; Interaction IA-41 names the same read `input_view = {hold_pm, armed, device_class, pressed_action}`. **Open:** one name, and `mode` and `armed` in the shape (HS-6 pins the union; OQ-HS-18) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| CF-HS-17 | **Inside Interaction:** V-6, ia:336, IA-15 (`CANCELLED`) and AC-IA-100 specify a cancel drain distinct from the abort snap, while IA-41 says `abort_cause` is diagnostic only and the HUD "uses one abort frame set for every cause". The Controller's older text ("holds then drains on a world abort") is a third variant | Interaction IA-41 vs Interaction V-6 / IA-15 / AC-IA-100; Controller UI Requirements | **Answered by `ended_by`:** the HUD never reads `abort_cause`; it draws the drain on `channel_view.ended_by` = `PLAYER_CANCEL` and the snap with break-notch on `WORLD` (HS-41; Interaction IA-41, registry `ended_by`) |
| CF-HS-18 | Panel geometry: the three specialists disagree (about 129 x 38 with 18-px cells; a bounding box of at most 100 x 44; a 4-row column), and none yet accommodates a 16-px glide needle | ux-designer vs art-director | Invariants only (HS-16); decided in `/ux-design` by the per-arrangement `offered()` sets of D.8 (ROW 144 x 40, COLUMN 74 x 80; the 100 x 44 box is withdrawn, re-review) |
| CF-HS-19 | **The mark under a lie:** Sanity SN-19 (sn:288; sn:1195, sn:1228) says the warning mark is TRUTH, driven only by Graph `alarm_eligible`, never shown or suppressed by a lie; the Suit (UI-3, se:1309) says the mark is "not an independent honest backup" and HS-30 says it inherits its reading's class; the Graph-driven reading uses the Suit read `suit.alarm`, accepted at MVP (HS-25, user ruling D89) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | Sanity SN-19 vs Suit UI-3 / HS-30 (routed to Sanity, Suit and the creative-director) | **Settled on the HUD side (stage 12; the creative-director's recommendation, confirmed 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20)*):** suit-rung marks inherit their reading's class; Graph-driven ship-alarm and world-housing marks stay honest (HS-30). Wording change routed to Sanity SN-19 (OQ-HS-61). R11 stands for the build; no Acceptance Criterion asserts Vertical Slice A mark-on-lie behaviour either way |
| CF-HS-20 | **Constant-name collisions with In-World Instruments (designed):** Instruments registers `INSTR_SETTLE_MS` 400 and `INSTR_SETTLE_ZETA` 0.7 as its own constants and `DIAL_ARC_DEG` 240 for world dials; this GDD proposed `SETTLE_MS` 300 for the suit dials | this GDD vs Instruments (iw:735) | This GDD renames its own knob `HUD_SETTLE_MS` 300 (the damping knob `HUD_SETTLE_ZETA` was retired 2026-10-27, design review, D.6) and its strip dial sweep `STRIP_SWEEP_DEG` 180, so no name has two owners; no locked value is touched; Instruments confirms it does not intend one shared settle constant (iw:241 treats `HUD_SETTLE_MS` as a peer; closed, OQ-HS-24) |
| CF-HS-21 | HUD needs the escalation state without counting; SL publishes FAILED per write and no count | Save/Load SL-21 / sl:739 vs HS-46 | **Closed:** Save/Load publishes `fail_escalated` (bool, SL-21 host-local read, registry `save_status`); the HUD draws the notice while it is true (HS-46) |
| CF-HS-22 | Suit se:99 / se:1155 / UI-2 name glide as the lie motion of every suit reading; numerals cannot glide | Suit vs Filter PF-18 / HS-28 | The HUD follows the Filter; Suit refresh owed (Refresh-owed table, OQ-HS-10) |
| CF-HS-23 | **What the strip's breath dial shows while the reserve is engaged:** Suit SE-30 and its door-breach timeline put the suit O2 (the reserve) on the strip's dial and demote the ambient numeral; the HUD kept the ambient reading on the strip permanently and never answered Suit OQ-41 | Suit SE-30 / OQ-41 vs HS-11, HS-12, HS-17 (re-review) | **Resolved 2026-10-27 — re-review, G1 and G2 (Suit is the published owner; creative-director lean, Option A):** the strip shows the reserve while `breath_src` is RESERVE, with a non-moving frame discriminator and the ambient numeral in the panel's ambient slot (HS-12); no user ruling is reversed (D90 covers marks only) |
| CF-HS-24 | **The panel cannot fit its own envelope:** HS-16 bound the panel to 100 x 44 while its content needs about 144 x 40 (ROW) or 74 x 80 (COLUMN) | HS-16 vs D.8, the offered() floor invariant | **Resolved in D.8 (re-review):** a budget, a `fit_v` / `fit_notice` and an `offered()` row per arrangement; the choice is `/ux-design`'s (OQ-HS-81); no budget constant was moved to hide the loss |
| CF-HS-25 | **The input-driven class could exceed the ceiling:** HS-47 counted input events as never deferred, so the promise that no player input could push the HUD past its flash ceiling (EC-HS-41, Game Feel) was false | HS-47 / D.2 vs EC-HS-41 | **Resolved (re-review):** class A state displays share one coalescing limiter; class B acknowledgement cues are rate-gated and may overrun, recorded as `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN`; both sentences restated |
| CF-HS-26 | The Controller's re-review wanted a `give_up` plate, an `input_ignored_view` dim on every reject including NOT_LIT, and first-use captions *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* | HUD vs Controller (AC-PCI-024 vs AC-HS-12, AC-HS-143) | Plate accepted (HS-42(b)); MOVING tell accepted via `press_blocked_moving`; NOT_LIT dim and captions refused; the Controller conforms (delegated CD ruling, 2026-10-28; K43) |

## Acceptance Criteria

Every criterion is GIVEN / WHEN / THEN, cites the rule that it verifies, and carries a tag. Ids are `AC-HS-n`; rule ids are `HS-n`. The two are **offset** (for example AC-HS-13 verifies HS-11), so a story must cite the **rule id**, never the AC number alone. The criteria were drafted from the rules, reviewed by the qa-lead (95 drafted; 9 tag corrections; 24 rewrites; 29 added), and the seven GDD defects the review found (F-1 to F-7) were fixed in the rule text before this section was closed.

**Tags.** **(auto)** gdUnit4 headless: a pure `HudMath` function, a layout function or a scripted fixture, in `tests/unit/hud/` or `tests/integration/hud/`. **(auto-windowed)** *(added 2026-10-27, design review)* a gdUnit4 test that needs a rendering device (draw calls, primitives, drawn-pixel colours, pixel-identical frames): the headless CI command cannot run it, so it runs in a separate **render lane** (a windowed job on a machine with a GPU) and **also has a headless oracle**, a *scene-state hash* (the ordered list of visible HUD nodes with atlas region, rectangle, modulate and visibility) that the headless lane asserts on every push, so the regression is caught even when the render lane runs nightly. Tags written `(auto)` in the first draft of a pixel or draw-call criterion are corrected to this one. **(lint)** a static or scene-tree scan of source, data, scenes or atlas names. **(review)** a reviewer reads text or data and records a line in the evidence file. **(manual)** a launched build observed by a person, with a **retained screenshot in `production/qa/evidence/`** (a parse check is not a run; a manual criterion is not closed until its screenshot is on disk). **(manual+auto)** the model is asserted headlessly and the look is proved by a retained screenshot; use it wherever the observable is visual. **(auto, provisional)** written against the HUD's proposed Adapter or Suit read and re-based when the owner answers the ASK. **(spike)** a measured prototype whose result is recorded in Open Questions with PASS or FAIL and a named fallback; a spike does not gate a story. **MVP** = live at MVP; **RESERVED** = specified and built, dormant until Vertical Slice A or B (driven by a stub Filter or stub seat in tests). "THIN fixture" = a compartment with `p_o2` inside the THIN band and an honest suit. **No criterion asserts what a Vertical Slice A lying reading does to its warning mark, in either direction** (CF-HS-19, CF-HS-15).

**Mode and defaults** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Unless a criterion says otherwise, the mark and arbiter criteria run with `reduce_flashing` and `reduced_motion` OFF: the fixture overrides the first-launch default of user ruling D79 so that the mark pulses and the arbiter is in WEIGHTED mode. Criteria that need the strict ceiling name STRICT; AC-HS-38 and AC-HS-110 cover the first-launch default itself.

**Evidence mapping.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: a HUD test-support prerequisite epic.)* **No HUD story can be tested until the test-support epic exists:** the stub Suit, Filter, Interaction, Controller (`confirm_view`, `glyph_view`) and Save/Load, the kernel clock with a scripted `(T, tick_alpha)` pinner, the fixture runner, the scene-state hasher and the pixel-colour scanner; every HUD story depends on it (OQ-HS-68). Logic criteria (formulas, the arbiter, the state machines): `tests/unit/hud/`, BLOCKING. Integration criteria (stub Suit, Filter, Interaction, Save/Load; presenter order): `tests/integration/hud/`, BLOCKING. UI and Visual criteria: retained screenshots in `production/qa/evidence/` for every surface and state listed in "Evidence plan" below, BLOCKING and not waived at `qa.level: minimal`. Config/Data (`hud_tuning` range validation): automated here although the standard makes it ADVISORY.

### A. Identity, layer and reads (HS-1 to HS-10)

- **AC-HS-1 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN the kernel registry dump after boot with the HUD loaded WHEN the registrations attributed to the HUD owner are listed THEN the list is exactly one `PRESENTATION` subscriber and zero hooks, state blocks, commands, mutators, RNG streams and `INPUT_SURFACE` subscribers; and a boot with the HUD removed still passes registry validation (the HUD is in no hook's `after:` list). (HS-1)
- **AC-HS-2 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN HUD source, data and scenes WHEN scanned THEN there is no `true_value` call site and no reference to it outside the Debug Overlay allow-list; the HUD calls `perceived(...)` only (Filter AC-PF-51; Debug Overlay P-1). (HS-4)
- **AC-HS-3 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the HUD source WHEN scanned THEN no code draws a number from `view()`'s four reading fields (`o2_pp_dkpa`, `reserve_pm`, `cell_pm`, `integrity_pm`) (Suit AC-SE-77). (HS-5)
- **AC-HS-4 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the HUD source WHEN scanned THEN the HUD reads none of `node()`, `edge()`, `abort_cause`, `interaction.state`, `failures()`, Graph state, `lit(ref)`, `peer_id`, Attention, Horror or Sanity state, or any `PERCEPTION_OUT` block (the read table of HS-6 is closed). (HS-6)
- **AC-HS-5 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the HUD scene tree and data WHEN scanned THEN there is no Overlay control in any HUD state: no HUD node hosts, instantiates or parents a Debug Overlay node, no HUD surface carries a debug toggle, and `hud_debug_snapshot()` is read-only and carries no `k`. (HS-57; Debug Overlay P-1)
- **AC-HS-6 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the HUD surfaces, strings, glyphs and data WHEN scanned THEN there is no sanity surface (no sanity number, bar, ring, icon, colour or glyph) and no Attention, noise, light, illumination, exposure, `lit_state`, `exposed` or `exposure` indicator, no horror, threat or proximity indicator, under any setting. (HS-10)
- **AC-HS-7 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the forbidden-surface list (minimap, compass, objective, off-screen arrow, crew locator, room name, ship status, weight or capacity, health bar, damage number, hit flash, vignette, tint, toast, banner, popup, death screen, score, autosave glyph, "repaired" confirmation, a countdown to an event the HUD names (the reserve numeral of HS-17 is a reading, not such a countdown, and is allowed), spinner, Alarm Red text, ring or radial progress, UI cursor in `gameplay`; the one exception, the opt-in `hud_aim_indicator`, may exist only while `aim_indicator` is on, AC-HS-134) WHEN the HUD scene tree, string table and atlas names are scanned THEN none exists. (HS-10, HS-52)
- **AC-HS-8 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the HUD tree WHEN scanned THEN every HUD `CanvasLayer` is layer 2 in group `hud` under the root viewport, no `Control` or `hud`-group node is under the world `SubViewport` (Pipeline lint (b)), and `Control.scale` is (1, 1) everywhere. (HS-2, HS-9)
- **AC-HS-9 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the HUD tree WHEN scanned THEN there is no `draw_line`, `draw_arc`, `draw_polygon` or `draw_circle` call, no node with non-zero rotation, no `offset_transform_*` use, and every bar, stroke and arc is a filled rectangle or an atlas region (4.7 lines have no feather). (HS-9)
- **AC-HS-10 (auto, MVP)** (a) GIVEN the kernel `PAUSED` and `present_time` frozen WHEN 600 render frames are drawn THEN no HUD timer value (settle, grid, debounce, bar fill or drain, tick phase) changes and every active mark draws ON solid on all 600 frames, whatever `phase_pm` the frozen `(T, alpha_pm)` gives (HS-23). (b) GIVEN the Chart Table open in solo pause WHEN process time advances 2 s THEN the Chart Table animates and the HUD model is byte-identical before and after. (HS-8; the step-clamp case is AC-HS-97)
- **AC-HS-11 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN each item of the forced-rebuild list (first `RUNNING` frame, restore, `ALL`-coalesced notification, exit from `PAUSED`, change of `H`, locale or glyph device) WHEN it occurs THEN the discrete model is rebuilt even with no dirty key; GIVEN no forced item and no `S/<PlayerId>` change THEN the discrete model is not rebuilt, and the five `perceived` records, `focus_view` and `channel_view` are still pulled every render frame. (HS-7)
- **AC-HS-12 (lint, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: retagged from review to a scripted comparison)* GIVEN the surface inventory table of HS-10 and the scene tree dump WHEN a script lists the HUD's top-level surface nodes and the reads each node's data binding names THEN the set of surfaces equals the table's (`hud_strip`, `hud_suit_panel`, `hud_rail`, `hud_prompt` and `hud_bar`, `hud_give_up_plate` (bound reads exactly `confirm_view` and `glyph_view`) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, `hud_notice`, the conditional `hud_aim_indicator`, the inspect-card frame, the reserved anchors) with no extra node, and each node's bound reads equal its row's "Source read" column (the strip's `perceived(suit.reserve)` and `breath_src` included). (HS-10)

### B. The critical strip (HS-11 to HS-15; D.1, D.5, D.6, D.15)

- **AC-HS-13 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN the layout function WHEN `strip_budget` is evaluated at the 1080p reference THEN `W_content <= 76`, `H_content <= 18` and both slacks are non-negative; and GIVEN `W_arc` is increased by 16 (a 16 x 16 O2 glyph added) THEN `slack_w` is -3 (content 79 against the cap 76) and the budget check returns FAIL. (D.1, HS-11)
- **AC-HS-14 (manual, MVP)** GIVEN a launched build at 1920 x 1080, `H` 3 WHEN the strip is captured THEN it shows `[dial arc with pivot cap][numeral][mark slot]` left to right, no O2 loop glyph, inside 76 x 18 HUD px, and the mark slot is present and empty at rest; screenshot retained. (HS-11)
- **AC-HS-15 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN a THIN fixture (honest suit, `breath_src` ROOM, `p_o2` 175 dkPa, `O2_DIAL_MAX_DKPA` 300) and a stub wall gauge reading 210 in the same compartment WHEN the strip is drawn at steady state THEN the drawn numeral is 175 and equals `perceived.displayed_value`, `view.o2_pp_dkpa` and the compartment's `p_o2`; the dial `pm` is 583, `theta` is 104.9 degrees (accept 104 to 105), and the needle frame is `(583 * 34 + 500) div 1000` = **20** of 35 with `theta_frame` 105.88 degrees and the drawn tip (15, 4) at `L` 11 and the pivot at (12, 15); for 210 dkPa: `pm` 700, `theta` 126 degrees, frame **24**, `theta_frame` 127.05 degrees, the pure function's target tip (18, 6) and the **drawn** tip (19, 6) (D.5 and D.15 define them separately, so the two are asserted on their own functions, not against each other) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; and the stub gauge does not change any HUD value. (HS-12, HS-14, D.5, D.15; the "second instrument" narrative is AC-HS-94)
- **AC-HS-16 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `displayed_value` 0, 99, 100, 175, 210, 300, 301, 999, 1000 and 1500 WHEN the digit-cell model and needle are computed THEN the cells are `[blank, blank, 0]`, `[blank, 9, 9]`, `[1, 0, 0]`, `[1, 7, 5]`, `[2, 1, 0]`, `[3, 0, 0]`, `[3, 0, 1]`, `[9, 9, 9]`, `[9, 9, 9]`, `[9, 9, 9]` (leading zeros suppressed, right-aligned, clamped to 999), the needle `pm` clamps at 1000 above 300, and nothing exceeds three columns. (HS-12, EC-HS-13)
- **AC-HS-17 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN HUD data and source including the dial-generator script WHEN scanned THEN there is no dkPa band literal for the O2 zone edges; each edge is read by name (`O2_BAND_ANOXIC_PP`, `HYPOXIA_PP`, `O2_BAND_THIN_PP`). (HS-13)
- **AC-HS-18 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `HYPOXIA_PP` set to 120, 140 and 160 WHEN the dial is regenerated THEN the HYPOXIC tick moves to 72, 84 and 96 degrees (`STRIP_SWEEP_DEG` 180, `O2_DIAL_MAX_DKPA` 300); `O2_BAND_ANOXIC_PP` 100 and `O2_BAND_THIN_PP` 180 give `edge_theta` 60 and 108; the tick, not the band width, is the asserted information (a band under 3 px of arc is not relied on). (HS-13, D.5)
- **AC-HS-19 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a stub that raises `suit.o2` by 1 dkPa every 6 ticks for 2 s WHEN the honest numeral is driven THEN it changes only on ticks with `T mod 30 == 0` (the grid is anchored to the committed tick and never drifts), at most `1000/HONEST_TICK_MS` times in any rolling second (never more than 3), shows the latest value at each boundary, skips intermediate values (never queued, never faded, **never delayed to a later tick**), and equals the stub's final value within `2 x HONEST_TICK_MS` of the last change. **Replays:** GIVEN the same stub at a render rate of 30 fps, and GIVEN a 250 ms hitch (the render frame skips 15 ticks, then `dt` is clamped) in the middle of the run, THEN the sequence of displayed values at the boundaries is identical to the 60 fps run's, no boundary is drawn twice, and no window of 60 ticks holds more than 3 HUD events with the mark running (every event in STRICT mode, where the mark is solid and adds none; the counted events only in WEIGHTED mode, where the swaps are exempt). The sequence of displayed values is identical in both modes. (HS-14, HS-47) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HS-20 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; this criterion tests the pure function `hud_settle_response`, not the drawn needle, which is quantised to HUD px and frame)* GIVEN a step from rest of 200 permille (36 degrees) WHEN the propagator is advanced at 60 fps with `HUD_SETTLE_MS` 300 THEN `p` never passes the target (no overshoot), is monotone, `|e| / |e0|` is at most 0.02 (plus a 1e-4 float tolerance) at 300 ms and at every frame at or after it (0.019999 at 300 ms, 0.0151 at frame 19), and `p` stays inside 0-1000; GIVEN `reduced_motion` THEN `p = g` on the first frame after the target change. (D.6, HS-14)
- **AC-HS-21 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; replaces the retired damping-ratio check)* GIVEN `HUD_SETTLE_MS` below 250 or above 400 WHEN the data file is validated THEN validation fails; GIVEN any `HUD_SETTLE_MS` in range THEN `omega * HUD_SETTLE_MS / 1000` equals `K_SETTLE` (5.834) and no NaN or infinite value appears for `dt` of 0, 1, 16.7, 50 and 10,000 ms (the last clamped). (D.6)
- **AC-HS-129 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a target staircase falling 1.2 dkPa (4 permille) every 6 ticks (a ramp of -12 dkPa/s, 40 permille/s) for 5 s with the sub-frame rule disabled (the pure-function lane) WHEN the propagator is advanced every 16.7 ms THEN `p` is non-increasing at every frame (no backward motion), the steady lag is within 4.1 permille plus one 4-permille step of the target, and no frame restarts the settle. GIVEN the same ramp through the drawn-needle lane THEN the needle frame index is non-increasing and differs from the target's frame by at most 1. (D.6 P3, P4)
- **AC-HS-130 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a step in progress at 0.1 s WHEN the target changes again THEN `p` and `v` are bit-identical immediately before and after the retarget (position continuous, velocity kept), and the subsequent response equals the closed form from the new `(e, v)`; GIVEN a retarget that reverses direction THEN at most one turn-around occurs and `p` stays continuous. GIVEN the same elapsed 300 ms advanced as 6 steps of 50 ms, as 18 steps of 16.667 ms and as 1 step of 300 ms called directly on the pure function (the clamp is applied by the caller, not the function) THEN the three `p` agree to 1e-6 permille (the semigroup property). GIVEN a change smaller than `FRAME_PM` while at rest THEN no settle starts (`v` stays 0); GIVEN the reference ramp (4 permille every 6 ticks) from rest with the sub-frame rule **enabled** THEN `p` is unchanged for the first 7 steps (28 permille owed), the settle starts on the 8th step (32 permille owed, `|e| >= 29.4`), `p` is then non-increasing and follows the ramp within the P4 lag plus one `FRAME_PM`, and the drawn needle's frame index changes at least once in every 1.0 s of the ramp (no frozen needle) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. (D.6 P1, P2, P5)
- **AC-HS-22 (manual, MVP)** GIVEN the Steam Deck configuration (1280 x 800, `H` 3, `k` 2) WHEN the strip is captured and cropped THEN its left and right edges, reported in HUD px, give a centre offset within 1 HUD px of the canvas centre (expected left edge 175 HUD px, screen x 526, plate width 76 per D.9); the measured width is recorded as a percentage of 426.67 HUD px (expected about 17.8 % for the 76-px plate; the content width is about 15 %) and attached for the art-director's confirmation of the accepted overage; screenshot retained. (HS-15, D.9)

### C. The suit panel (HS-16 to HS-21; D.3, D.11, D.12, D.14)

- **AC-HS-23 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `displayed_pm` 0, 1, 11, 12, 333, 500 and 1000 and `RESERVE_S` 90 WHEN `reserve_seconds` is computed THEN the results are 0, 1, 1, 2, 30, 45 and 90; GIVEN `RESERVE_S` 240 THEN 1000 permille prints `240` in three cells; the numeral is 0 only at exactly empty. (D.3, HS-17)
- **AC-HS-131 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN `RESERVE_S` 90 and `RESERVE_WARN_S` 30 WHEN the reserve underlay is laid out THEN `warn_notch_px` is 6, the notch is drawn in the frame register at every reserve value and never moves, and a reserve of `displayed_pm` 333 fills 6 of 16 px, exactly to the notch (the numeral reads 30); GIVEN `RESERVE_S` 240 and `RESERVE_WARN_S` 20 THEN the notch is 2; GIVEN the forbidden-surface lint (AC-HS-7) THEN the reserve numeral and its underlay pass it (a plain reading of the suit's own store is not a countdown to an event the HUD names). (HS-17, D.3)
- **AC-HS-133 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN an active mark in the OFF phase at `T mod 128` = 100 and the kernel toggled `PAUSED` / `RUNNING` 20 times in 2 s (process time, with fewer than 64 ticks of `RUNNING` in all) WHEN the arbiter counts mark edges THEN there is at most one **pause-induced** mark leading edge per `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` of running time (here one for the whole run) (the first pause entry; edges at natural multiples of `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` are counted separately), no pause-induced trailing edge on any resume (the mark stays ON until the next leading edge), and no window of 60 ticks holds more than `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` HUD events; GIVEN the same run in an ON phase THEN zero mark edges are added by the toggling; GIVEN `reduced_motion` THEN zero. (HS-23, EC-HS-9)
- **AC-HS-24 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: a concrete fixture, so it can fail on its own)* GIVEN `RESERVE_S` 90, a stub reserve at 100 permille rising 100 permille per second for 3 s (the top-up verb) WHEN the numeral is driven THEN the values drawn at the boundaries `T mod 30 == 0` are exactly 9, 14, 18, 23, 27, 32, 36 (`reserve_seconds` of 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 350, 400 permille, D.3), no value is drawn on any other tick, the sequence is strictly increasing (it counts upward in whole steps), no value is tweened, faded or queued, and at most 2 numeral changes fall in any 60-tick window. (HS-17, EC-HS-16)
- **AC-HS-25 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN the integrity dial data WHEN the zone ticks are computed THEN they sit at the angles for `SUIT_HP_OK` 7000, `SUIT_LEAK_HP` 5000 and `SUIT_HP_FAIL` 2500 (126, 90 and 45 degrees at the default `PANEL_SWEEP_DEG` 180; 168, 120 and 60 at 240) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; and a lint on the data finds neither Graph `HP_OK` nor `HP_FAIL`. (D.11, HS-18)
- **AC-HS-26 (lint, MVP)** GIVEN the integrity and warmth cells WHEN scanned THEN no numeral node exists on either (no health number and no temperature number). (HS-18)
- **AC-HS-27 (review, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the UX spec's panel layout and `HudLayout`'s rectangles for the chosen arrangement WHEN reviewed THEN (i) the authored needle length of each of warmth and integrity is a number in the spec and is at least `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` 16; (ii) the panel rectangle returned by `HudLayout` at every offered `H` has `y_top >= strip_top + 18 + GAP_MIN_PX` or `x_right <= x_strip - GAP_MIN_PX`, and no intersection with the chest keep-out; (iii) the panel's rectangle and the notice stack's rectangle both lie outside the bottom-centre band `[x_strip - GAP_MIN_PX, x_strip + 76 + GAP_MIN_PX]`; each is a number in the review line. (HS-16 (a) to (e))
- **AC-HS-28 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `displayed_pm` 1000, 500, 126, 125, 16 and 0 and `CELL_FILL_PX` 8 WHEN `charge_fill_and_warn` is computed THEN `fill_px` is 8, 4, 2, 1, 1 and 0; GIVEN `CELL_ENABLED` 0 THEN the cell is an empty slot with no disabled art. (D.12, HS-19)

### D. The warning mark (HS-22 to HS-25; D.4)

- **AC-HS-32 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `T mod 128` and `alpha_pm` sweeps WHEN `mark_phase` is computed THEN `phase_pm` is monotone within a period and resets at the boundary, ON iff `phase_pm < 500`, the leading edge is at `T mod BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS == 0`, and all marks return the same phase; with `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` varied across 120 to 180, `MARK_PERIOD_TICKS` is never a second value. (D.4, HS-23)
- **AC-HS-33 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the model observable `mark_active(slot)` and the draw observable `mark_drawn_ON(slot)` are asserted separately, and the phase offset is a parameter)* (a) For each of the five suit-rung slots, a stub rung table at STRAINED has `mark_active` false and the slot empty (neither the ON fill nor the OFF outline); at CRITICAL before `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` the same; at CRITICAL at `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` `mark_active` is true in that slot's own rectangle and the slot draws ON or the OFF outline according to the phase (never empty), run at phase offsets `T mod 128` of 0, 63, 64 and 127; the mark rectangle does not intersect the reading's numeral or dial rectangle. With `breath_src` `RESERVE` the breath rung makes `mark_active` true in both the strip slot and the reserve-cell slot (HS-22). (b) The charge cell shows a mark at `CELL_WARN_S` remaining and not one tick earlier (the stub Suit publishes the state; the HUD must not compare to `warn_pm`). (c) FAILED and DOWNED keep the mark; a restore into DEAD shows none. (d) Lint: no mark-related code compares a `displayed_*` field to a numeric threshold. (e) *(User ruling D89; **provisional** — written against a key the Perception Filter has not yet defined, OQ-HS-79; it runs against a stub key and is re-based when the Filter answers; re-review)* The ship-alarm slot: GIVEN a stub `suit.alarm` false, then true, then false WHEN the panel is drawn THEN the alarm slot is empty, then draws ON or the OFF outline by the shared phase (never empty while true), then is empty on the render frame it goes false; it is driven by `perceived(suit.alarm)` only (lint: no `failures()`, `alarm_eligible`, Attention phase or loop id is read), draws the same asset on the same phase as the other five, is unaffected by any stub lie (TRUTH, pinned `never`), and names no loop or place. (HS-22, HS-24, HS-25) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HS-34 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a mark that becomes active (`mark_active` true) just past its duty WHEN the phase is stepped THEN it is drawn ON (`mark_drawn_ON`) at the next leading edge, showing the OFF outline until then, with worst-case latency `(1000 - MARK_DUTY_PM)/1000 * BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` ticks (64 at defaults); GIVEN it becomes active inside its duty THEN it is drawn ON at once; the test is parameterised over the activation offset `T mod 128` in 0, 1, 63, 64 and 127. (D.4)
- **AC-HS-35 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN a rung dropping from CRITICAL mid-pulse WHEN the next render frame is drawn THEN the slot is empty on that frame (hide is never deferred) and the OFF outline is not shown. (HS-22, EC-HS-23)
- **AC-HS-36 (manual, MVP)** GIVEN a launched build with a CRITICAL breath axis and a scripted fixture runner that feeds the HUD's `PRESENTATION` subscriber a fixed `(T, tick_alpha)` while the kernel state stays `RUNNING` (not `PAUSED`, so the pause hold of HS-23 does not apply; no tick-step is needed) at `T mod 128` = 10 (ON) and at 80 (OFF) WHEN the strip is captured in each THEN the ON slot contains Alarm Red `#E0102A` pixels and a Paper White stroke; the OFF slot contains zero Alarm Red pixels and a 1-px Graphite-lit chamfered outline of the same 16 x 16 slot; the strip rectangle is pixel-identical outside the mark slot between the two shots (no reflow); and no Alarm Red text exists; both screenshots retained. (HS-22)
- **AC-HS-37 (manual, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the Suit has three axes plus the charge band, so "five" means five mark slots, produced by `rung_breath` CRITICAL with `breath_src` `RESERVE` (strip and reserve slots), warmth, integrity and the charge warn point; six with `suit.alarm`, accepted by user ruling D89)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN six simultaneous marks (stub Suit plus a true `suit.alarm`) with the scripted fixture runner pinning `(T, tick_alpha)` in `RUNNING` in the ON and OFF phases WHEN captured THEN the ON shot has at most 1,536 HUD px of Alarm Red (6 x 16 x 16; at `H` 3 the raw screen count is at most 13,824, or block-centre sampled as in D.7) and at least 1 HUD px per mark, the OFF shot has 0, the six slot rectangles show the same state in each shot (shared phase), and at rest the Alarm Red area is 0; screenshots retained. (EC-HS-22)
- **AC-HS-38 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `reduced_motion` or `reduce_flashing` WHEN a mark is active THEN it holds ON solid with no edges; GIVEN a fresh `HudSettings` (reduce flashing ON at first launch, user ruling D79) THEN an active mark is solid from its first frame. (HS-23, EC-HS-25) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HS-39 (review, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Promoted from a draft by user ruling D89)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: stated as greps with a pass line)* GIVEN the Suit GDD and the HUD tree WHEN a grep is run THEN the Suit GDD contains `alarm_bit` and `suit.alarm` as a one-bit TRUTH-class reading with its lie stage pinned `never`, and its SE-30 states a maximum of six marks; the HUD scene tree has exactly one alarm-slot node, whose only data binding is `perceived(suit.alarm)`; and the HR-8 gap (reduced motion plus a deaf player) is recorded closed in this GDD (CF-HS-4). The Filter's own definition of the key is OQ-HS-79 and is not part of this criterion's pass line. (HS-25, CF-HS-4)
- **AC-HS-125 (auto + manual, MVP)** GIVEN a CRITICAL breath axis and the scripted fixture runner pinning `T mod 128` = 80 (OFF) in `RUNNING` WHEN the kernel enters `PAUSED` THEN from the first paused render frame the mark slot draws the ON state (Alarm Red fill, Paper White stroke), the Graphite-lit OFF outline is never drawn while paused, the arbiter records exactly one mark leading edge for the pause entry and none while paused, the HUD is otherwise byte-identical to the pre-pause frame, and a retained screenshot shows the held-ON mark beside the Chart Table pause panel; GIVEN no active mark WHEN paused THEN the slot is empty (no OFF outline); GIVEN `reduced_motion` on THEN the pause adds no edge; WHEN the kernel resumes THEN the mark stays ON until the next leading edge (the resume bridge, HS-23, AC-HS-133) and then follows the unchanged `T` with the clamped step. - **AC-HS-44 (auto, RESERVED)** GIVEN a panel needle of `L` 24 and a stub GLIDE record with 2.2 px wander at 0.40 Hz WHEN run for 10 s THEN the selected frame index changes at least once in every 1.0 s window (`NEVER_REST_MAX_S`); GIVEN the strip needle (`L` 11) THEN it is reported ineligible to glide and the O2 lie uses the numeral plus the gleam. - **AC-HS-46 (review, RESERVED)** GIVEN a lying reading WHEN reviewed THEN the HUD never draws the true value beside it, never shows a lie count, and never caches a `Perceived` across frames (Filter must-nots). (HS-4)

(d) The glowstick count is 0 to 4 in Paper White (assert no pixel equals Chemlight `#86B896`) and decrements on the tick the throw command is accepted, not on landing (fixture: accept at tick t, assert the model at t + 1 with the stick still in flight). (e) The carried-module glyph and the `G` / pad `X` keycap are present if and only if a module is carried. Screenshots retained for (a), (b) and (d). - **AC-HS-50 (manual, MVP)** GIVEN a launched build with a scan result WHEN the scanner slot is captured twice 250 ms apart during a result THEN it contains Paper White numerals or a band only, no Alarm Red, no green or red hue pixels, no icon other than the 16 x 16 scanner glyph, and the slot's frame pixels are identical across the two shots; the "reading register" look (thin, light, moving marks inside a still frame) is the art-director's sign-off recorded as a separate review line that **does not decide this criterion's pass or fail**, which rests on the pixel checks alone; screenshots retained. - **AC-HS-51 (review, MVP)** GIVEN the scanner slot's lie gate WHEN reviewed THEN it is `SCANNER_LIE_STAGE` (never by default, stage 3 only), not `READING_LIE_STAGE`. (HS-33)
- **AC-HS-52 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN the Adapter's glyph device switching twice within `DEVICE_SWITCH_HYSTERESIS` THEN the glyph set swaps once; GIVEN a non-IDLE channel or a non-zero hold THEN it does not swap until IDLE and the next prompt show; the swap takes at most one frame, shows no popup, and the HUD never reads `InputEvent.device` or compares to `0`. *Criteria added by the QA review (AC-HS-96 onward) sit in the topical group they belong to, so ids are not strictly ascending within a group.*

### Additions to the reads and the clocks (HS-4, HS-8; EC-HS-10, 12, 17, 18)

- **AC-HS-96 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `perceived(SUIT_KEY(k))` returns no record for key `k` WHEN the frame draws THEN that reading's plate draws with a blank numeral and the arc at rest, `HUD_READ_MISSING` increments, no other reading is affected, and the HUD never reads `view()`'s reading fields as a fallback; GIVEN `displayed_pm` -50 or 1500 or `leak_amplitude_pm` -1 or 1200 from a stub THEN values clamp to range and `hud_perceived_oob` increments. (HS-4, EC-HS-17, EC-HS-18)
- **AC-HS-97 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN a time step of 0, 50, 51, 500 and 10,000 ms WHEN fed to every presentation timer THEN the applied step is `min(dt, HUD_DT_CLAMP_MS)`, no value is NaN or infinite, no latch, debounce, settle or tick fires from the gap, and the honest grid is re-armed after a pause, focus loss or hitch. (HS-8, EC-HS-10)
- **AC-HS-113 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN no committed tick yet WHEN the HUD is asked to draw THEN nothing is drawn, `view()` is not called, the mark phase is 0 and no mark exists (UNMOUNTED). (EC-HS-12)
- **AC-HS-117 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN a 30 fps render rate and a mark edge between render frames THEN the edge is drawn once on the first render frame at or past the boundary; GIVEN a hitch of 2 x `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` ticks THEN the mark shows the current phase and draws no catch-up pulses. (EC-HS-11)

- **AC-HS-100 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the Suit's published edges 333 / 466 / 533 / 666 WHEN `warmth_band_index` is computed for `displayed_pm` 0, 332, 333, 465, 466, **500**, 532, 533, 665, 666 and 1000 THEN the zone is 0, 0, 1, 1, 2, **2**, 2, 3, 3, 4 and 4, **at `displayed_pm` 500 (the nominal reading) the `WZ_COMFORT` zone is the lit one and no other zone is**, and the four notch positions equal the published edges (59.9, 83.9, 95.9 and 119.9 degrees at 180); GIVEN the Suit retunes the edges THEN the notches move and no edge literal exists in HUD data or source (lint); the five zones are pattern-coded in Paper White ink with no hue pixels outside the palette; GIVEN a missing or non-increasing edge read THEN no notch is drawn, `hud_perceived_oob` increments and the data-load lint fails. - **AC-HS-101 (manual + auto-windowed, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a state sweep (all readings OK; each loop source stopped; each rung from OK to FAILED) WHEN the HUD is rendered in each state and its pixel histogram taken THEN the palette set is the HS-20 list only; Power Amber, Coolant Ice and Fuel Ember pixels count 0; the integrity glyph is Hull Grey; no reading's ink colour differs between its OK and CRITICAL state; a glyph's silhouette hash does not change with state; a retained screenshot set and the histogram are filed. - **AC-HS-103 (auto, RESERVED)** GIVEN a stub GLIDE record WHEN the host reading does not draw (no scanner equipped, or `CELL_ENABLED` 0) THEN no gleam draws; the gleam edge never intersects a digit cell rectangle. (HS-27, EC-HS-49)

### G. The prompt plate and the channel bar (HS-37 to HS-45; D.16, D.18, D.19)

- **AC-HS-54 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a focus that is stable for 12 then 13 ticks WHEN the plate is evaluated THEN it does not show at 12 and shows at 13 provided `since_last_show >= 20`; GIVEN `prompt_state` HIDDEN or `focus_ref` changed THEN the plate hides in the same render frame; GIVEN a **replay** of focus changing every 12 ticks for 10 s THEN **no plate is drawn on any render frame** (not for 50 ms), and GIVEN a replay of 11-tick churn THEN likewise none; no more than three plates start in any rolling second (Interaction AC-IA-72); GIVEN an accepted press on a focus stable for 5 ticks THEN the plate shows on the press frame. (D.18, HS-37)
- **AC-HS-55 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the fixture is stated)* **Fixture (the pure function in STRICT mode with the mark's edges injected to exercise the arithmetic; (d) is WEIGHTED):** a stub kernel at `TICK_RATE` 60 with `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128 and `u` = 1; the honest numerals are static in (a) (no grid event) and falling at every boundary in (b); the show-start requests are post-debounce (the debounce is AC-HS-54's subject) and **only show-starts are injected, with no hide events, so no pair completes and no orphan hide is counted** (the hide cases are AC-HS-142) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the mark is active from tick 0 with its leading edges at multiples of 128; the arbiter clock is the committed tick. (a) GIVEN show-start requests every 10 ticks from tick 0, filtered first by D.18 (`PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` 20), with a mark edge scheduled at tick 128 WHEN `flash_admit` runs THEN the admitted show-starts are at 0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100 and 140, and in every run no half-open 1000 ms window contains more than 3 HUD luminance events. (b) GIVEN numerals falling at every boundary at `HONEST_TICK_MS` 500 and one mark WHEN run for 60 s THEN the long-run admitted prompt show-starts are at most 0.53 per second (about one per 1.9 s, leading-edge unit) and the maximum luminance events in any window is 3. (c) Positional events are not counted: a 40-px bar filling in one second adds 0 events. (d) *(WEIGHTED mode, user ruling D91 (iv))* GIVEN the same show-start requests at 1080p `H` 3 (ENABLED plate, weight 34) with the mark edge at tick 128 WHEN `flash_admit` runs THEN the requests filtered by D.18 are all admitted, at 0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100, 120 and 140; those at 0, 20, 60, 80, 120 and 140 are exempt and those at 40 and 100 are counted (their cumulative weight reached 102); in no window do the counted events plus the mark edge exceed 3; GIVEN the same requests at 720p `H` 3 with a NEEDS_TOOL plate (weight 115) THEN none is exempt and the result equals (a). (D.2, HS-45) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HS-127 (auto + manual, MVP)** GIVEN the reference breach fixture (numeral falling on every `HONEST_TICK_MS` boundary, the mark pulsing at `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128, a valve entering focus and staying stable) WHEN `flash_admit` runs over 20 s of simulated ticks THEN the prompt show-start (no press) is admitted at most `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS + HONEST_GRID_TICKS` ticks after focus is stable when at most one other counted event is in the window (13 + 30 = 43 at the defaults, 717 ms; typically the debounce alone, 13 ticks, 217 ms, when only the two grid events are in the window; AC-HS-144 asserts both) and at most 13 + `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 60 = 73 ticks in any case, and 0 frames after an accepted press on an ENABLED or NEEDS_TOOL subject *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, the prompt is admitted ahead of the grid boundary that would breach `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` (that boundary is skipped and the next shows the then-latest value), no window of `TICK_RATE` ticks holds more than `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` leading-edge events, and the mark edge is never delayed; WHEN the breach fixture is played by a tester THEN the tester records, for each valve prompt, whether it was perceived as late (the playtest hook OQ-HS-59). **Mode** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: the figures above are STRICT mode (the first-launch default, the mark's edges injected into the pure function); in WEIGHTED mode the same fixture shows the plate at `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS` (13 ticks, 217 ms) at 1080p `H` 3 (ENABLED weight 34) and skips no numeral boundary. (HS-45, HS-47, D.2, Game Feel)
- **AC-HS-144 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — re-review; the first-launch STRICT breach, end to end)* GIVEN a fresh `HudSettings` (STRICT, the mark **solid**, no injected edges, **no plate drawn or hidden in the 60 ticks before the valve becomes stable**, so no previous plate's orphan hide is in the window), the breach fixture (`breath_src` ROOM to RESERVE at tick 380, the honest numerals falling at every boundary) and a valve entering focus stable at tick 100 (before the swap) and again at tick 400 (inside the swap's window) WHEN `flash_admit` runs THEN (a) the **typical** latency, valve stable at tick 100 with only the two grid events in the window, is **13 ticks** (the plate is drawn at tick 113); (b) the **worst-case** latency, valve stable at tick 400 with the swap event and the grid events in the window, is **at most 43 ticks** and the numeral's boundary is skipped once and shows the then-latest value on the next, so the numeral's lag never exceeds 1000 ms; (c) with a second hue cut added the plate is drawn at most 73 ticks after stable focus and `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN` increments only if it was force-admitted at the bound; (d) a press at tick 405 (accepted, ENABLED) draws the plate and the ACK on tick 405 itself. (HS-45, HS-47, Game Feel step 3; the figures replace "717 ms is the first-launch latency")
- **AC-HS-135 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — user rulings, D91 (iv))* GIVEN the pure functions `flash_weight_pm` and `exempt` WHEN evaluated THEN a six-cell numeral change (210 px) weighs 9 at 1080p `H` 3 (canvas 640 x 360), 19 on the Deck (`keep`, 426 x 240, the same as 720p `H` 3) and 33 at 1080p `H` 6; the ENABLED plate (864 px) weighs 34, 60, 94 and 135 at 1080p `H` 3, 4, 5 and 6 and 77 on the Deck and at 720p; the NEEDS_TOOL plate (1,296 px) weighs 51, 90 and 141 at 1080p `H` 3, 4 and 5 and 115 on the Deck and at 720p *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: one Deck picture mode)*; a two-line plate (2,160 px) weighs 85 at 1080p `H` 3 and 150 at `H` 4; GIVEN STRICT mode (`reduce_flashing` on) THEN no event is exempt and every HS-47 event counts; GIVEN WEIGHTED mode THEN only the DIGIT_SWAP and PROMPT_SHOW_START classes can be exempt, an Alarm Red mark edge is never exempt, and the cumulative guard returns an event to `admit` when its weight plus `X(t)` reaches `FLASH_AREA_EXEMPT_PM`; GIVEN `FLASH_AREA_EXEMPT_PM` 101 THEN data validation fails. - **AC-HS-56 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN a chest point at HUD (x, y) and anchors A1 on top of the chest point (no AD-5 placement clears the 16-px keep-out), A2 two px outside the picture edge, and A3 inside the strip rectangle WHEN drawn THEN A1, A2 and A3 all draw no plate (never clamped); `UI_PLACEMENT_WITHHELD` increments for A1 and A3 and not for A2 (off screen is not a withheld placement); no plate rectangle, including the `give_up` plate *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* and the self plate (AC-HS-150) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix; delegated ruling G6, GAP)*, ever overlaps the chest keep-out or the strip, panel or rail rectangles. - **AC-HS-60 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN SHORT (`L` 16, 48-tick hold at the default; an adjusted hold, D80, changes only the ticks per pixel, since the fill follows `hold_pm`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* THEN `progress_pm` 500 gives 8, 1000 gives 16, 62 gives 0 and 63 gives 1; GIVEN MEDIUM (`L` 24, 90-tick cap) THEN `progress_pm` 333 gives 7 and 1000 gives 24; GIVEN LONG (`L` 40, 240-tick cut) THEN 0, 250, 500 and 1000 give 0, 10, 20 and 40; and the HUD draws whichever `length_class` it is given and never recomputes it (a channel at exactly `BAR_LONG_FROM_TICKS` 240 is LONG and at 239 is MEDIUM, Interaction's call). - **AC-HS-61 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a press WHEN accepted (`confirm_view.pressed_action`) THEN the keycap drops 1 art px and the track appears at fill 0 on the same render frame (ACK), **and a plate that was still inside the debounce, the minimum cycle or an arbiter wait is force-admitted on that frame** (HS-37; the arbiter counts it as non-deferrable); GIVEN no result within `PREPLAY_MAX_TICKS` THEN the pre-play is withdrawn; GIVEN a result event of kind REJECTED THEN the bar is withdrawn within one render frame and the restored keycap is held Graphite-lit for `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS` with at most one reject presentation per seat per `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS`, with no shake, flash, red or text, identical for every reason, and a reject with no plate shown draws nothing. - **AC-HS-63 (manual+auto, MVP)** GIVEN each state ABORT, DONE and OUTCOME WHEN drawn THEN (auto) ABORT with `ended_by` `WORLD` snaps the fill empty with a 2 x 2 Ground break-notch for `BAR_HOLD_DONE_TICKS` 12, the same frame set for every world cause, and ABORT with `ended_by` `PLAYER_CANCEL` drains the fill over `BAR_DRAIN_MS` 400 with no notch (AC-HS-65); the HUD never reads `abort_cause` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; DONE holds full 12 ticks then cuts with no checkmark, flash or "repaired" text; OUTCOME fills exactly as DONE then removes the bar and steps the plate border to Graphite mid for 12 ticks with no text; (manual) screenshots of each are retained. - **AC-HS-70 (manual+auto, MVP)** (auto) GIVEN a fixture in which `save_status` reads FAILED on failures 1 and 2 with the escalation flag false, and the flag true on failure 3 (`AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT`) WHEN drawn THEN failures 1 and 2 draw no notice; on failure 3 the 16 x 16 record-slot glyph and the one phrase (`notice_save_failed`) appear at the suit panel's edge, persistent across 600 frames with pixel-identical frames (no animation), no cause, no Alarm Red, Flesh or Violet, cleared by the next WRITTEN; WRITING and WRITTEN draw no per-write glyph, sound or text; FAILED, WRITTEN, FAILED inside one render frame shows the notice (last committed value) and adds one show-start event only; with the first-frame line also shown the line rectangle is directly above the notice rectangle, both inside the suit panel's edge, and neither intersects the bottom-centre band; screenshots retained. - **AC-HS-71 (manual+auto, MVP)** GIVEN the first `RUNNING` frame after a fallback load WHEN the Chart Table has not shown it THEN the UI-7 line appears on a Ground plate above the notice slot, for `UI7_DWELL_S` 8 on `present_time`, then cuts; GIVEN the Chart Table has shown it THEN the HUD shows nothing; screenshot of the line retained; the `DEATH_STUB` trigger and the precedence rule are AC-HS-136. - **AC-HS-72 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN each of the deferrable priorities (in STRICT mode; in WEIGHTED mode the exempt classes of AC-HS-135 leave the order) WHEN several are pending THEN hides and mark edges are never deferred, Filter ticks are never rescheduled, the class-B acknowledgement cues (force-admitted show-start, ACK and reject keycap, OUTCOME border) are counted, rate-gated and never deferred, the class-A state displays (bracket move, count substitution, lamp glyph, TAP relabel) go through the one shared limiter (at most one drawn change per `GLYPH_STATE_LATCH_TICKS` across the class, latest state shown, force-admitted and counted at `MAX_DEFER_TICKS`), and among HUD-owned deferrable events the order is prompt show-start, honest grid boundary (skip-only, never skipped twice in a row), notice or scanner show-start, then the state-display class *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; GIVEN a prompt waiting with a full window THEN the next grid boundary is skipped (never delayed) and the numeral shows the then-latest value (lag at most `2 x HONEST_TICK_MS`); GIVEN the scanner slot's show-start and a full window THEN it waits and is admitted before the lamp-glyph change; GIVEN the kernel `PAUSED` THEN no deferrable event is admitted.
- **AC-HS-73 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` 128, 120 and 180 WHEN `honest_tick_floor_ms` is computed for `u` = 1 THEN the results are 396, 400 and 375 (the average-rate floor), and any `u` other than 1 fails data validation (the edge unit is retired, user ruling D91 (ii)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; GIVEN a grid of `g` ticks, `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` and `u` WHEN the validator runs the window-feasibility check of D.2 THEN `g` = 30 passes at `u` = 1 at 120, 128 and 180, `g` = 24 fails (three boundaries plus a mark edge in one 60-tick window), and `HONEST_TICK_MS` other than 500, or a live `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS` for which 30 ticks is infeasible, fails data validation. (D.2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HS-74 (auto, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: rewritten after the deferral rule landed in HS-47, so the final-state clause is now derivable)* (a) GIVEN the lamp toggled ten times in one second (an odd or an even number of toggles, run both) in STRICT mode with no other HUD event WHEN the charge-row glyph is drawn THEN the world beam toggles on every toggle, the glyph changes at most once per `GLYPH_STATE_LATCH_TICKS` 20 and at most 3 times in any 60-tick window, and at the last tick of the burst plus `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 60 the drawn glyph state **equals the simulation's lamp state**; (b) GIVEN an **even** number of toggles ending on the starting state THEN the glyph draws no net change (a draw of the state already shown is not a luminance event) and the arbiter counts none for it; (c) GIVEN the window held full by non-deferrable events THEN the pending draw is force-admitted at `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` and counted, and `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN` increments only then. (HS-19, HS-47, EC-HS-28)
- **AC-HS-140 (auto, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — re-review; blocker 2, the first-launch default)* GIVEN `reduce_flashing` ON (STRICT) and the HUD at rest, WHEN tool cycling at 10 Hz (the bracket and Tungsten move on every change) is combined with lamp toggles at 10 Hz and glowstick throws at 5 Hz for 10 s THEN in every 60-tick window the HUD holds **at most 3** state-display draws and **at most 3** luminance events in total (no other event is present), each draw shows the simulation's latest state, and at 60 ticks after the last input the drawn bracket, glowstick count and lamp glyph all equal the simulation state; GIVEN the same run with the honest numerals falling at every boundary (2 events a second) THEN the state-display draws take only the remaining room, no boundary is skipped twice in a row, a draw that has waited `MAX_DEFER_TICKS` is force-admitted and counted, and `HUD_FLASH_OVERRUN` is incremented exactly by those forced draws and by nothing else; GIVEN the TAP confirm armed and disarmed 10 times in a second THEN the relabel follows the same limiter and ends on the simulation's armed state. - **AC-HS-145 (auto-windowed, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — re-review; the contrast floors, which no criterion measured)* GIVEN the retained captures of the strip, panel and rail in every state of the state sweep of AC-HS-101 WHEN a script samples the foreground and background pixels of each glyph, state-carrying stroke (the lamp-OFF outline, the mark's OFF outline, the dimmed dkPa notches in RESERVE, the dead-source arc band) and each text or numeral pixel run THEN the contrast ratio of every glyph and state-carrying stroke is at least 3:1 and of every numeral and text run at least 4.5:1; **the criterion is expected to FAIL for Graphite lit `#5C6670` on Ground (about 2.8:1)** until the art-director and accessibility-specialist either move those strokes to Hull Grey or record a waiver, which is OQ-HS-83; the failure is reported by name, not hidden by a threshold. (c, auto) An unmapped rebound key renders the wide `?` keycap, increments `hud_unmapped_key_glyph`, and the plate width does not change against a mapped key. (d, auto) A +40 % pseudo-locale string wraps to at most two lines at `PROMPT_PLATE_MAX_W_PX` 72, never truncates, never shrinks the face, and stays outside the exclusion region; a third line grows the plate and increments `hud_plate_overflow`. (HS-50)
- **AC-HS-78 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN the layout function WHEN called with `(picture, H)` THEN it is a pure function of `(picture rect, H)`, the safe margin is `(5*P + 99) div 100` per axis converted with a ceiling (1920 x 1080 `H` 3 gives 32 x 18; 1280 x 720 `H` 3 gives 22 x 12; `H` 6 gives 11 x 6), and the reserved anchors `crew_strip`, `chat`, `caption` and `own_identity` exist and draw nothing. A single failing display is reported by name. (D.8, D.9, HS-54, HS-55)

### I. States and transitions (States and Transitions; HS-53)

- **AC-HS-84 (auto + scene-state hash, MVP)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: "pixel-identical" is asserted headlessly as an identical scene-state hash, and in the render lane as identical pixels)* GIVEN a restore WHEN the first `RUNNING` frame draws THEN the readings are at the restored values with no settle, no abort frame and no notch, every presentation-local counter (debounce, grid, bar fill and drain, tick phases, flash history, device latch) is cleared, the mark draws from the live `T`, and the HUD frame after a restore with `restore_reason` = DEATH_STUB differs from one with any other reason only by the record line of HS-46 (the first-frame line rectangle, shown for a fallback load or for `DEATH_STUB`, user ruling D15), everything else identical. (HS-53) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HS-85 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN solo pause WHEN rendered THEN the HUD is held undimmed (no dim, blur or desaturation), readings freeze, an active mark is held ON solid (HS-23), and the first post-resume `dt` is at most `HUD_DT_CLAMP_MS` with a forced discrete rebuild. (HS-8, HS-53)
- **AC-HS-86 (auto, MVP)** (a) GIVEN DOWNED THEN readings keep updating, the lamp stays lit and marks persist with no banner and exactly one node added, the HS-42(b) `give_up` plate (while `give_up_live`) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. (b) GIVEN DEAD THEN after the seat dies 600 frames show an identical model, and a scene-tree diff before and after death shows no added node (no death, score, fade, banner or text beyond the standing surfaces). (c) GIVEN `TRANSIT_HOLD` THEN the HUD's layout is unchanged and the readings keep updating from `perceived` (statuses run in the pod, user ruling D60); GIVEN the seat dying in the pod THEN the HUD enters DEAD (readings freeze, no death screen). (HS-53) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HS-132 (integration, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — design review; extends AC-HS-84)* GIVEN a stub kernel and a stub Save/Load WHEN the seat becomes `DEAD` and the stub performs the SL-7 sequence (staging at the `DEAD` boundary, the epoch write polled for one or more frames, `request_restore` with `DEATH_STUB`, kernel `LOADING`, then `RUNNING`) THEN the HUD (a) draws no new node during `DEAD` (including the epoch-staging frames) and `LOADING` (scene-tree diff) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: R14)* and holds its last model, (b) pulls no `perceived` record or `view()` while `LOADING`, (c) on the first `RUNNING` frame draws the restored values directly with no settle (`p = g`, `v = 0`), no abort frame and no notch, clears every presentation-local counter, draws the one record line of HS-46 in the existing line slot (the only new visible element, AC-HS-136), draws a mark ON at once only if the live phase is inside its duty and the restored rung is CRITICAL, and (d) needs no player input and shows no dwell screen at any step (the record line of HS-46 blocks nothing and cuts after `UI7_DWELL_S`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. GIVEN no loadable candidate (`SL_LOAD_NO_LOADABLE_SAVE`) THEN the HUD stays in its last model and draws nothing new while the Chart Table's boot-flow screen takes over. (HS-53, States and Transitions)
- **AC-HS-136 (integration, MVP)** *(Added 2026-10-27 — user rulings, D15 / D84; extends AC-HS-132)* GIVEN a stub kernel and a stub Save/Load WHEN a restore with `restore_reason` `DEATH_STUB` reaches its first `RUNNING` frame THEN the HUD shows exactly one reading-face line (`line_record_restored`) in the line slot above the notice slot for `UI7_DWELL_S` 8 s on `present_time`, then cuts; a review of the string table finds no death wording (no "dead", "died", "death", "lost"), no count and no cause; whether the Chart Table showed anything changes nothing; GIVEN the fallback flag is also set THEN only the fallback line shows (one line, no queue); GIVEN any other `restore_reason` and no fallback flag THEN no line shows; and every presentation-local counter is cleared as in AC-HS-84. (HS-46, HS-53)
- **AC-HS-87 (auto, MVP)** GIVEN `H` changes mid-channel THEN the layout is recomputed and the prompt re-projects, the bar's fill is recomputed from `progress_pm`, no simulation value is read or written, and the transition is a cut (the SCALE_CHANGE state); GIVEN a zoom step mid-channel THEN **no layout is recomputed** (every HUD rectangle is unchanged), only the prompt plate re-projects, the bar's fill is recomputed from `progress_pm`, nothing enters SCALE_CHANGE, and the transition is a cut. (EC-HS-1, EC-HS-2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

The bar is **provisional**: a miss is a recorded spike FAIL with a named fallback (bake the dial faces once per layout, cut primitives, simplify the plate set), not a failed story, and a PASS is the evidence that ratifies the figures. GIVEN the full MVP HUD live at once (six marks ON *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, a prompt plate with a LONG bar at fill 20 px, the failed-write notice, the first-frame line, the scanner slot with a reading, the rail at full counts) on RM-DECK at the 1280 x 800 `keep` display configuration (`H` 3 over `k` 2) and on RM-DESKTOP at 1920 x 1080, each on the R12 release export build, WHEN measured over 600 frames after the pipeline's 120-frame warm-up *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)* THEN HUD draw calls are at most 60 (the Pipeline's `D_hud` ceiling, cited), submitted primitives at most `HUD_PRIMITIVE_MAX` 90, HUD update cost at most `HUD_FRAME_BUDGET_US` 400 microseconds at the 95th percentile, and zero per-frame heap allocations in steady state (the `perceived` reads fill reusable caller-owned records, HS-4; the dial faces are baked once per layout, HS-9) and an atlas plus needle-strip footprint at most `HUD_MEM_CEILING_MB` 8 `[PH]` (unmeasured; no budget existed before this review). The 400 figure is unmeasured: a miss logs the measurement and raises the S2 question. (D_hud; spike S2)
- **AC-HS-93 (spike)** (S1) GIVEN 1280 x 800 (`keep`), `H` 3 over `k` 2 with the **manual snap in `HudLayout`** (the default, HS-9) WHEN 20 HUD controls are measured THEN each global rectangle position is a whole multiple of `H` plus the `extra_x` split (this half passes or fails on the manual snap alone); and GIVEN the same run with `gui_snap_controls_to_pixels` set true THEN the property's observed effect on the HUD `CanvasLayer` is **recorded** (applies, does not apply, or is viewport- or project-scoped `[UNV]`) as a spike result, not as a pass line, because the claim that it snaps HUD controls cannot be asserted against the pinned reference *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the fallback is the manual snap, already the default. (S3, S4) GIVEN a 76-frame 24-px needle strip (101 frames at the 240-degree sweep) at `H` 3 and 6 over a 60 s run with drift 1.19 degrees/s plus wander 2.2 px at 0.40 Hz THEN the tip pixel changes at least once in every 1.0 s; the same test at `L` 11 fails by design (ineligible). ### Evidence plan (retained screenshots in `production/qa/evidence/`)

Strip at rest, THIN, vacuum, mark ON and OFF; the strip in RESERVE (full, mid, at the warn notch, empty) in colour and in greyscale with the ambient slot lit, beside the ROOM and GASP strips (AC-HS-137), and the notice stack at the smallest offered `H` of each arrangement (AC-HS-82) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; a held-ON mark beside the Chart Table pause panel with a CRITICAL fixture (AC-HS-125); the avatar at each of the five facings with the lamp off (AC-HS-126); the breach fixture's prompt-latency capture (AC-HS-127); panel (reserve, integrity, warmth, charge on and off, a mark in each slot); rail (glowstick 0, mid and full; the empty fourth cell; carried module; pad set; keyboard set); prompt (verb, NEEDS_TOOL, carry set); bar (SHORT, MEDIUM, LONG; ACK, ABORT, DONE, OUTCOME); failed-write notice; first-frame line; scanner slot; the layouts at the offered `H` pairs of 1280 x 720, 1280 x 800 `keep` and `expand`, 1920 x 1080, 2560 x 1440, 3840 x 2160 and an ultrawide; the at-rest non-Ground capture with its count; CVD and achromatopsia captures of the strip, panel and mark; the ship-alarm slot ON and OFF; the record line after a death restore; the opt-in aim indicator at several of the 16 steps *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; the `give_up` plate (fill 0, mid-hold, TAP armed) for both glyph sets and at each placement fallback, in `production/qa/evidence/hud/` (AC-HS-147) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; the self plate ("Patch suit") at each offered `H`, each facing and each placement fallback (AC-HS-150) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix; delegated ruling G6, GAP)*. Visual sign-off items (the settle's look, the mark pulse, the reading-register look, 16 x 16 glyph legibility) are recorded by the creative-director and art-director on the retained captures.

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **AC-HS-79 and AC-HS-82 were blocked on the CF-HS-11 option; no longer (design review 2026-10-27).** The MVP offers exactly `offered(P_w, P_h)` (D.8, option (a) as a stopgap), so both iterate that closed table. What remains open is which panel arrangement (ROW 144 x 40 or COLUMN 74 x 80, D.8) the UX spec's feasibility sketch chooses; both tests iterate the closed table once for each arrangement until it does *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HS-65, AC-HS-61's pressed-state half and AC-HS-66's TAP half depend on the Controller's `confirm_view`** (shape agreed in HS-6; the `mode` and `armed` fields are published by the Controller as of 2026-10-28, the single name and field names still to reconcile, CF-HS-16, OQ-HS-18 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*); Interaction's `PLAYER_CANCEL` answer has arrived as `ended_by` (CF-HS-17). Until the Controller accepts the union, they run against a stub of the HUD's shape. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **`HUD_FRAME_BUDGET_US` 400 is unmeasured**, pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12 named the machines and the build; OQ-HS-87 closed) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*; AC-HS-92 is a spike until S2 closes. The HUD atlas and pre-baked needle strips (76 frames at `L` 24 per dial at the 180-degree default, 101 at 240) have **no memory budget** stated anywhere; none is invented here (Open Questions).
- **Vertical Slice A behaviour is deliberately untested**: no criterion asserts what a lying reading does to its rung mark (CF-HS-19; the split is ruled in HS-30 but waits for Sanity's wording and the Filter's mark field), and the charge-cell lie signature (CF-HS-14) has no test beyond "a slot exists".
- **Warmth band edges** (AC-HS-100) test the Suit's published edges (SE-29: 333 / 466 / 533 / 666 at the defaults; the equal-spacing placeholder is retired, EC-HS-20); what stays owed is the Suit's confirmation that an edge value belongs to the upper zone (OQ-HS-70) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the entry had kept the retired placeholder)*.
- **Subjective qualities** (whether the settle reads as a calm instrument; whether the mark is alarming without frenzy; text legibility of a 5-cap keycap letter) cannot be pass or fail; they are lead sign-offs on retained screenshots and first-playtest questions.
- **Right-to-left fill direction, CJK and Arabic coverage** are open (HS-49) and have no criterion.
- **Counter semantics** for `UI_MISSING_KEY` (once per distinct key per plate show) are a ruling made here and should be confirmed by the UX spec.

## Open Questions

Ids are `OQ-HS-n`. Columns: **owner** is `user` (a decision only the user can make), `agent` (a specialist or the producer), or the GDD or role that owns the answer; **target** is where it lands (`playtest`, `next GDD`, `ADR`, `UX spec`, `art bible`, `spike`). The `user` rows come first; every other row is an ASK with its landing document so none is lost. Nothing in this GDD waits on an answer except two items, both named where they occur: the HUD's TAP-mode story, which waits on the Controller's story that lands `mode` and `armed` in its confirm read (published in the Controller GDD 2026-10-28; names still to reconcile, OQ-HS-18) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, and `/ux-design`, which waits on the drawn feasibility sketch (OQ-HS-34); every other row has a stated default that the GDD already uses. Rows OQ-HS-7, 65, 66 and 67 were USER DECISIONS and are **resolved by user ruling 2026-10-27** (each row carries its resolution line); OQ-HS-76 to 78 carry what the rulings leave open. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* Rows that restate a Cross-References conflict cite its `CF-HS` id. Every row about an undesigned or in-flight neighbour is labelled provisional.

(iv) **Whether `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` applies per area (WCAG 2.3.1 weights a flash by the area it covers).** Today the strict global ceiling also throttles 1-px digit swaps and the 12-px prompt plate, which are far below the area threshold, and it costs up to 1,000 ms of O2 numeral lag and a 717 ms valve-prompt wait at the anchor breach moment (the Challenge aesthetic and P2). (v) Whether `reduced_motion` should solidify the mark pulse (a flash effect, not a motion effect), and what a stricter arbiter under `reduce_flashing` is (the table now separates the two settings by what they change). **Creative-director lean:** exempt sub-threshold digit swaps and prompt show-starts by default; keep the strict global ceiling for the Alarm Red mark and under `reduce_flashing`; separate `reduced_motion` (settle, glide, drain) from `reduce_flashing` (mark SOLID plus a stricter arbiter). **Superseded by the ruling above:** the strict global ceiling counting every event of HS-47's inventory now applies in STRICT mode (reduce flashing, the first-launch default). | user (resolved); accessibility-specialist for the written requirement | art bible; accessibility requirements; `hud_flash_budget` |

The user resolves which body cues ship in MVP; the playtest then runs **two arms**, with sound and muted, each judged against the same pass criterion, and a muted-arm failure with a sound-arm pass is recorded as an audio-cue gap, never a HUD defect. | user (resolved) | game concept / systems index; playtest plan *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |

The pulse (P2, one rhythm shared with the beacon) and the area exemptions exist only after a player turns reduce flashing off. **Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20): accept this as the cost of a safe default.** Alternatives: let `reduced_motion` alone govern the pulse, or split the setting at the Pipeline so that the HUD mark is not throttled by the world-flash default. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | closed (R20) | Chart Table / Pipeline / Adaptive Audio |
